# v0.7 to v0.8 migration instructions

For coding agents (Codex or similar) that port Wordflow v0.7 behaviour into v0.8. Written 2026-10-03 and last updated 2026-10-07 from branch `v0.7` of Australian-Text-Analytics-Platform/ldaca-wordflow, range `aaea1c3b..a6ca90d4` (2026-09-24 to 2026-10-07), up to pre-release 0.7.11. What 0.7.11 adds since release 0.7.10 is listed under **Changes in 0.7.11** below. The human-readable summary is [v0.7-branch-changes.md](v0.7-branch-changes.md).

## How to use this document

1. **Reference code.** The v0.7 code at commit `a6ca90d4` is the reference implementation. Read any file with `git show a6ca90d4:<path>`. When an entry below and that code disagree, the code wins, except where an entry says the code has a known gap.
2. **Order of precedence.** (a) The cross-cutting rules R1 to R12 apply everywhere. (b) Within an area, entries are in commit order and a later entry replaces an earlier one where they conflict. The most common conflict is a user-visible label; the **Label changes that replace earlier entries** section lists them. (c) Individual entries.
3. **Work entry by entry.** For every entry `V07-NNN`, record one decision:
   - **Port**: v0.8 has the same feature; carry the behaviour over.
   - **Reimplement**: v0.8 does it differently (for example DuckDB instead of Polars); meet the same *Expected behaviour* by other means. Entries flag Polars-only workarounds under *v0.8 notes*; do not copy those workarounds into DuckDB code.
   - **Not applicable**: v0.8 has no such feature, or already behaves this way. Give the reason.
4. **Tests are the contract.** Every bullet under *Expected behaviour (testable)* must become a test (unit, component or integration) or a written manual check if it cannot be automated. The *Tests* line names the v0.7 tests you can read for fixtures and edge cases.
5. **Safari.** Entries that mention WebKit or Safari must be checked in Playwright WebKit against the running app, not only in jsdom or Chromium. The product owner tests on Safari.
6. **Wording.** User-visible text follows rule R5. Quote labels exactly as the reference code has them.
7. **Report back** with a table: ID, decision, v0.8 commit, tests added, notes. Anything you cannot port, list with the reason; do not skip silently.

Do not change behaviour that an entry records as a deliberate decision (for example "Deletion is permanent", "every group starts ticked", "at most 50 Data Blocks per run") without asking the product owner, Chao Sun.

## Changes in 0.7.11

Release 0.7.10 (2026-10-05) ended at V07-227. These entries were added for 0.7.11 (pre-release, not tagged); port them like the others. The bigger features came with new cross-cutting rules: R8 empty values last, R9 ordered categories, R10 checked conversions, R11 elapsed time, R12 Topic Modelling on large corpora.

| ID | Area | Change | Issue |
|---|---|---|---|
| V07-228 | Data Builder | Filter: number between prefills the smallest and largest values; a plain-date end includes the whole day | #277, #311 |
| V07-229 | Concordance | Text mode: no L1 for a match at the start of a text | #312 |
| V07-230 | Concordance | L1/R1 tint prefers a whole word, ignoring case | #295 |
| V07-231 | Data Loader and files | One unfinishable import no longer marks the whole history corrupt | #310 |

| V07-234 | Topic Modelling | Every highlighted bubble opens its hover card during a search | #314 |
| V07-235 | Build, CI and tests | `pnpm dev` picks free ports for both halves | none |
| V07-236 | Trends | Axis picker offers only binnable columns; the backend checks the type | #316 |
| V07-237 | Tables and pagination | Empty values sort last in every table (rule R8) | #317 |

Sample data (#315): Honi Soit is a ZIP again and the Queensland election data CSV again (sample-data repository d9984da, a135fc0). v0.8 must load these original formats; no app entry.

## Label changes that replace earlier entries

Earlier entries quote the labels they introduced. These later changes replaced them. Use the final form.

- "Workspace" (every label) → "Project" (#132)
- "Add to Workspace" → "Add to Project" (#132)
- "Load" / "Unload" / "Upload project" → "Open" / "Close" / "Import Project" (#140, #205 item 25)
- "Data Preprocessing", "Preprocessing" → "Data Builder" (sidebar short name "Builder") (#144, 4d85698e)
- Sidebar "Data Loader", "Topic Modelling" → short names "Loader", "Topics" (full names in headers) (4d85698e)
- "Run All", "Clear Results" → "Run", "Clear" (4d85698e)
- Data Builder "Create Data Block" / "Create N Data Blocks" → "Add to Project" / "Add N to Project" (4d85698e)
- "Selected Data Blocks" → "Data Blocks (n/m)" (4d85698e)
- "Token Frequency Analysis", "Trends and Sequence" → "Frequency Analysis", "Trends Analysis" (4d85698e)
- "Tokenizer", "Topic Modeling" → "Tokeniser", "Topic Modelling" (4d85698e)
- "Change Project" → "Switch Project" (#206)
- Type labels "integer", "datetime", "categorical", "string", "float" → "whole number", "date and time", "category", "text", "decimal" (full list in R5) (#178, #206, #205 item 4)
- "null", "(empty string)" → empty cell, "(empty)", "(blank text)" (#176)
- "Failed to …", "Could not …", "Unable to …" (toasts and errors) → "Couldn't …" (#205 item 25)
- "data block", "block", "dataset", "node" → "Data Block" (#205 items 7 and 25)
- "Tick all" / "Untick all", "Select loaded" / "Clear" → "Select all" / "Select none" (#205 item 25)
- "Citation", "View citation" → "README", "View README" (#205 item 25)
- Date format panel "How are the dates written?", "Date format", "Detect from the data", examples list (V07-114) → the conversion window: "Formats that fit", "What each part of … is", "Format code (for advanced use)", "Read month first" (#322, V07-241)
- LDaCA row button "Download" → "Import" (#205 item 25)
- Title Case option names ("Use as Study Corpus", "Compare To", "Clear Selection", "Table View", "Create New", "Add Group", "Random Sample", "Text Column") → sentence case ("Use as Study corpus", "Compare to", "Clear selection", "Table view", "Create new", "Add group", "Random sample", "Text column") (#205 item 25 and 57c47fc5)
- Concordance "Uncased", "Bin No.", "Density: line" → "Ignore capitals", "Sections", "Line" (#205 item 14)
- Trends "Frequency", "X-axis: Categorical / Linear", "Chart Type: Line Chart" → "Period", "Spacing: Even (hide empty periods) / To scale (show gaps)", "Chart: Line / Bars / Area" (#205 item 21)
- Stop words "<Language> (Recommended)" → "<Language> (Detected)" (a04a742d)
- Classic stop-word list "Chinese (746 words)" → removed (no licence); the library's Chinese list remains (#119, 9b55ad7c)
- Tasks panel "Freq - 1", quick access "Frequency: 1" → "F-1", "Frequency: F-1" (#211)
- Running card "Task running" with "Task ID: …" → "Running", no Task ID (#205 item 25)
- Row Details header "Field" → "Column" (57c47fc5)
- Trends axis "2020-10-18" (Even) / browser-locale dates (To scale, tooltip) → "18 Oct 2020", "Oct 2020", "2020 Q4" in every spacing and the tooltip (#213, 915ed42d)
- #213 "Scrolling the mouse wheel or pinching over the chart does not zoom" → plain scroll scrolls the pane; Cmd (Ctrl elsewhere) + scroll zooms; pinch zooms graphs but not ECharts charts (#215, 914e6a80)
- Data Editor "Apply ... closes the tool; Cancel discards it" → Apply keeps the tool open on the same column; Cancel discards unapplied settings; Close closes (#217, ddc50f2a)
- Trends legend share "of all shown rows" (visible groups only; hidden entries showed "Hidden" without a share) → share of every group that meets the minimum group count, hidden ones included: "B (3 · 37.5% · Hidden)" (#219, b9e8e95e)
- Trends and Concordance "turn on Select range and drag across the periods/bins" → click and Shift-click only; hint under the chart (#224, 579ed077)
- #225 automatic stacking of Trends bars when groups did not fit → separate Bars and Stacked bars types; only the zoom cap is automatic (#226, 9be69503)
- A folder of texts (and a ZIP member) shares the single-file source size limit (64 MiB) → Bounded by the Data Block storage limit (1 GiB); single files have no size limit (#236)
- Concordance "Empty values come first in either direction" (V07-062, V07-213) → "Empty values (missing or blank) come last in either direction", in every table (#317, 2d4a0ace)

## Cross-cutting rules (apply everywhere in v0.8)

These rules came out of several issues each. They apply to every tool, not one screen. v0.8 must follow them from the start instead of rediscovering them.

### R1. Errors in plain words, technical text under Details (#205 items 1, 2, 17, 20, 22)

- Every error a user sees is a written sentence that says what went wrong and what to do. Titles start with "Couldn't …" (for example "Couldn't rename column "x"."). Never "Failed to", "Could not", "Unable to" or "Error performing".
- A failure with no written message shows: "Something went wrong in Wordflow. Try again. If it keeps happening, please send feedback with the details." (backend `UNEXPECTED_ERROR_MESSAGE` in `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/shared/errors.py`).
- Technical text (exception class and message, Polars errors) is never the main message. The backend puts it in `details.diagnostic` for 5xx responses and in `Failure.diagnostic` for failed background tasks (`domain/background.py`). The frontend keeps it as `ApiError.technical`.
- The UI shows the technical text only under **Details**. Toasts open an "Error details" dialog (shared host, `stores/errorDetailsStore.ts`, `components/errors/ErrorDetails.tsx`), because a sonner toast measures its height once and cannot hold an expanding panel. In-place notices (failed runs, the Tasks panel, the data folder setup) expand in place (`ErrorNotice`). Details offers **Copy details** and **Send feedback**; startup screens pass `feedback={false}` so the button is not shown twice.
- Validation errors (422) name each field in plain words, up to three: `validation_message(details)` gives sentences such as "Group by columns: choose each column only once."
- Provider failures (AI annotation) map to plain messages by code and provider: `provider_failure_message(code, provider)` in `domain/annotation.py`.
- File loading failures say why a file can't be read (`describe_load_failure(exc, filename)` in `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`).
- Silent failures are reported: every catch that used to log only now shows a toast or notice (#205 item 22).
- Frontend entry point: `toastError(error, fallback, { title })` in `frontend/src/lib/toastError.tsx`, backed by `presentError` / `presentFailureMessage` in `lib/errorPresentation.ts`.

### R2. Tables keep their scroll position (#208, #209)

Symptom (seen in Safari first, and in every analysis table): sorting, paging or updating a table reset the scroll of the whole results pane, sometimes only on the second sort.

Three causes, three rules:
1. `flexRender` from TanStack Table creates a new component type per render, so header and cell subtrees remounted and lost focus and scroll anchoring. Rule: render column parts with `renderColumnPart` (`frontend/src/lib/table/renderColumnPart.ts`). `flexRender` is banned by an eslint `no-restricted-imports` rule.
2. Swapping the rows for a "Loading…" row while a page loads shrinks the table. Rule: keep the old rows, dim them and make them non-clickable (`busyTableBodyProps`).
3. WebKit lays out in the middle of a React commit; a brief height drop clamps the scroll container. Rule: hold the wrapper's min-height through an update (`useStableTableHeight`, `lib/table/useStableTableHeight.ts`). The `overflow-anchor` idea was tested and does not fix it.
The issue body of #209 is the full record with verification steps.

### R3. One rule for every rename box (#210)

Applies to columns, Data Editor tabs, analysis tabs, the Project name and Data Blocks (graph card and sidebar dialog). Implemented once as `useInlineRename` (`frontend/src/lib/rename/useInlineRename.ts`).
- Enter submits, and always tries again after a failure. Esc cancels.
- A failed rename shows a toast and keeps the box open with the text selected (focus is taken back if the submit came from a blur).
- Blur with the text unchanged since the failure closes the box and keeps the original name. No second request.
- Blur with edited text submits it.
- An unchanged name closes without a request. An empty name is a failure ("Enter a name.").
- Contract: `onSubmit(name)` rejects, or resolves `false` after showing its own message, to keep the box open. Rename handlers must return their promise; before #210, Data Block and tab rename failures were silent because nothing awaited them.
- In v0.8 Data Block names are DuckDB table names, so clashes will be common. Route every rename through this contract.

### R4. Analysis tab names (#211)

- New tabs are named with the tool prefix and the next number: F-1 (Frequency), C-1 (Concordance), T-1 (Trends), TM-1 (Topic Modelling), Q-1 (Quotation), A-1 (Annotation). The Builder has no analysis tabs.
- Older default names ("3", "Analysis 3") display with the prefix. Renamed tabs keep their names. Numbering continues from the highest number used (after C-9 comes C-10). Another tool's prefix counts as a renamed name.
- The Tasks panel and quick access use the tab's own name ("F-1", "C-2 · Run", "Frequency: F-1").
- Code: `tabPrefix`, `displayTabTitle(name, kind)`, `nextTabTitle(names, kind)` in `frontend/src/features/views/common/analysisNavigation.ts`.

### R5. Wording rules (#205 decisions, items 3 to 25)

- Always "Data Block" and "Project" (never node, block, dataset, workspace). Keep "Study corpus" and "Reference corpus" as terms of art.
- Australian spelling: colour, favourite, analyse, modelling, summarise, normalise, behaviour, grey, initialise.
- Sentence case for labels, options and dialog titles. Agreed tool and panel names keep their capitals (Frequency Results, Row Details, Add to Project, Project Graph…).
- "…" (one character), never "...".
- "Select all" / "Select none" for every select pair.
- "column", never "field", for table columns (Row Details header is "Column / Value").
- The file is called "README" (not "citation").
- "Import" brings data into Wordflow; "Download" saves a file to the computer.
- No em dashes in help pages.
- One data-type vocabulary everywhere: text, whole number, decimal, true / false, date, date and time, time of day, length of time, category, list, list of text, topic coverage, other. The exact Arrow spelling appears only in a tooltip (`arrowTypeDisplayName`, `arrowTypeTooltip` in `frontend/src/lib/arrow/arrowTable.ts`).
- Dates display year first ("2020-01-30 14:05") in tables and Row Details, at display points only; decoded values stay ISO for charts and filters (`displayDateTime`). Charts use one fixed readable format instead ("18 Oct 2020", "Oct 2020", "2020 Q4"), never the browser locale (`lib/chartDates.ts`, #213).
- Task and progress messages in plain words ("Counting words…", "Saving the results…").

### R6. Safari / WebKit

Chao tests on Safari. Check every scroll, focus and layout change in Playwright WebKit against the running app, not only Chromium or jsdom. Known WebKit behaviours hit on v0.7: mid-commit layout clamping scroll (R2), tab strips over-scrolling (#157), Row Details document box not repainting until remounted (#162), source column scroll alignment in the Data Editor preview (#154), and ECharts SVG clip paths changed in place, which hides lines and areas until a repaint (#213; `EChartsView` re-inserts each clipPath after every render).

### R7. Help pages

- Source of truth: `frontend/public/{tutorials,information,references}` plus `frontend/src/tutorials/bundledRegistry.ts`. The `ldaca-wordflow-docs` repo is a mirror filled by `pnpm -C frontend docs:sync-publish`; versions publish from a mutable tag `v{major}.{minor}` (v0.7 published 2026-09-28, mirror c456457).
- ui.md has one plain section, "How Preview, Run and Clear work" (`help-ui-preview-run-clear`), linked from every analysis page. Tool pages must not explain the lifecycle in developer terms (snapshot, projection, Analysis forest, root, Supporting Analysis).

### R8. Empty values sort last (#317)

Agreed by Chao and Alex: in every table, a sort on a user-chosen column puts empty values last in both directions. Empty is the same idea as everywhere else (missing, NaN, or blank or whitespace-only text), so the key must turn NaN and blank text into NULL before `NULLS LAST`, for example `ORDER BY CASE WHEN is_empty(col) THEN NULL ELSE col END DESC NULLS LAST`. DuckDB puts NULLs last by default for ASC only, so say `NULLS LAST` explicitly. Ties among empty rows keep reading order where the table has one. Client-side sorts (the Keyness table) follow the same rule. Details in V07-237.

### R9. Ordered categories (#318)

A category column has an order, and every place that lists or sorts its values uses it: table sorts, Filter's value list, Group, Trends groups and axis, and the Topic Map colour legend. An unordered category counts as A to Z. Empty is never a category and always comes last. In DuckDB, store an ordered category as an ENUM, whose ORDER BY follows the declared order. Details and decisions in V07-238.

### R10. Conversions are checked before they run (#322)

Every type change that has to interpret values (text or category to a date or date and time, numbers to a date, text to a number, a date to text) opens one window: it shows how the values are read, a few rows with their result, and a check of the whole column (converted, would become empty, first failures) before **Convert**. Dates are read day first when both readings fit, with a one-click swap; two-digit years always ask for their century; Excel day numbers are offered but never preselected; the result stays in UTC. Rounding to a whole number is to the nearest, halves away from zero. Details in V07-241.

### R11. Elapsed time (#324)

Time into a recording (a transcript's start times) is its own type, "Elapsed time", separate from date and time and from clock times. It shows as `7:58.5` under an hour and `1:23:20.25` from an hour, the fraction only when not zero. Every place that shows, filters, groups, sums or bins values handles it, and nothing casts it to text with a plain cast (Polars cannot; DuckDB INTERVAL text is `00:07:58.5`, so format it yourself). Excel times without a date load as it. Details in V07-243.

### R12. Topic Modelling must finish on large corpora (#329, #330, #331)

A 26,000-article corpus has to finish on a laptop. Three rules: embed on the CPU with all cores (Core ML was about 5 times slower for MiniLM) and run HDBSCAN in parallel; Automatic segments pack neighbouring whole paragraphs up to Max tokens (Paragraph and Sentence never pack); and **Topic sampling** (off by default) clusters a sample of segments picked with the run's seed, then gives every other segment its nearest sampled segment's topic. The UI always shows the estimated segment count; above 150,000 it suggests sampling (clustering time grows about as segments^1.5: 47 s at 107k, 497 s at 501k on an M5 Pro). **Segments to sample** shows a grey suggestion (100,000) that Run uses, Tab fills and typing replaces, and the note names the seed, the smallest findable topic (Min topic size × segments ÷ sample) and links to `help-topic-modeling-topic-sampling`. Above about 5 million estimated tokens a first-run note sits under the Data Blocks (embedding about 36,000 tokens a second on the M5 Pro). Details in V07-249 to V07-251. Repeated runs with the same seed give very similar, not identical, topics: PaCMAP's parallel neighbour search is not bit-exact (measured on 0.6.2 and 0.6.3; sequential HDBSCAN did not change it; #333 lists the options). The help no longer promises identical topics; the seed still fixes the document sample and the Topic sampling segments.

## Entries by area

### Errors and messages

#### V07-106 Errors in plain words, with technical text under Details (#205 items 1 and 2)

- Commits: 1b3fd27a
- Area: global error handling (backend API error contract, worker failures, frontend toasts, in-tab errors, Tasks panel, Data folder setup)
- Type: bug fix + wording
- Problem / reason: item 1 [bug]: `parseApiErrorResponse` checked `formatErrorDetail(parsed.details)` before `parsed.message`, so any error whose `details` object had no "message" key was shown as raw JSON (storage quota `{"limit_bytes":...}`, "Analysis input is missing" with UUIDs, Stack column mismatch, topic count out of range, "Project revision is stale", corrupt Project). Item 2: 5xx errors showed "<DeepestClass>: <cause>", analysis/import failures showed "ValueError: …", validation errors showed pydantic's "Value error, …" with no field name. Agreed rule: plain language for HASS researchers; technical text under a "Details" toggle that says it helps the developers and asks the user to paste it into the feedback form.
- What changed:
  - Backend (`main.py`, `shared/errors.py`):
    - New `UNEXPECTED_ERROR_MESSAGE` = "Something went wrong in Wordflow. Try again. If it keeps happening, please send feedback with the details."
    - `_app_error_handler`: for status >= 500 the response message is the error's written message (or `UNEXPECTED_ERROR_MESSAGE` if the message equals the code); `details.diagnostic` = `format_exception_diagnostic(exc)` ("Type: text") is added only when the message is the generic one or the exception has a cause/context. Below 500 the written message is sent as before.
    - `_unexpected_error_handler` and `_http_error_handler` (>= 500): message `UNEXPECTED_ERROR_MESSAGE`, diagnostic in `details.diagnostic`.
    - `_validation_error_handler`: message from `validation_message(details)`: up to three sentences "{Field label}: {message}." where the label is the last named location part with underscores as spaces and a capital first letter, pydantic's "Value error, " and "Assertion failed, " prefixes dropped, then "({n} more setting(s) to check.)"; empty list gives "Some settings are not valid. Check them and try again." (old: "Request validation failed").
    - Workers: `SupervisedProcessError` carries `user_message`; the child's error envelope has a 5th element; only an `AppError` with status < 500 and a written message (not equal to its code) passes its message through. `analysis_execution.py` and `user_file_imports.py` fail with `exc.user_message or format_exception_diagnostic(exc)`.
  - Frontend:
    - `ApiError.technical` (new) collects: `details.diagnostic`, the other `details` as pretty JSON, "Error code: {code}", "HTTP status: {status}", "Reference: {request id}". `parseApiErrorResponse` prefers the written `message` (ignoring one equal to the code or the legacy "Request validation failed"), never shows a details object as JSON, and no longer appends "(Request ID: …)" to the message (option `includeRequestId` removed).
    - `lib/errorPresentation.ts`: `presentFailureMessage(message, fallback)` treats a message matching `/^[A-Za-z_][\w.]*(?:Error|Exception|Interrupt|Exit|Warning)(?::\s|$)/` as a diagnostic: shows `UNEXPECTED_ERROR_MESSAGE` with the text under Details; `presentError(error, fallback)` handles ApiError/Error/string.
    - `lib/toastError.tsx`: `toastError(error, fallback, options)` replaces `toast.error(error.message)` across about 35 files; with technical text it adds a "Details" link and extends duration to at least 15 s.
    - `components/errors/ErrorDetails.tsx`: `variant="inline"` is a "Details" disclosure (chevron) that expands in place; `variant="dialog"` is a "Details" link that opens the single shared "Error details" dialog (`ErrorDetailsHost` in `GlobalHosts`, state in `stores/errorDetailsStore.ts`), because toasts measure their height once, capture the pointer for swipe-to-dismiss and may time out. Both show the explanation "These details help the Wordflow developers find the problem. To report it, copy them into the feedback form.", the text in a `<pre>`, and buttons "Copy details" (becomes "Copied") and "Send feedback".
    - `components/errors/ErrorNotice.tsx`: in-place error box (`role="alert"`) with Details; used for failed Concordance and Topic Modelling runs.
    - Tasks panel: a failed task's message goes through `presentFailureMessage` with inline Details (`TaskFailure`); Run block results use the plain message too.
    - Data folder setup form (`DataRootSetupForm`): plain message plus inline Details.
    - Help ui.md: "When something goes wrong, the message says what happened in plain words. Many messages also have **Details** … Use **Copy details**, then **Send feedback**, and paste the details into the form."
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A 500 from an unhandled exception shows "Something went wrong in Wordflow. Try again. If it keeps happening, please send feedback with the details." with a Details link; the dialog shows e.g. "ValueError: …", "Error code: internal_server_error", "HTTP status: 500", "Reference: <request id>".
  - An error with `details` but no `details.message` never shows JSON as its message.
  - A validation error for `number_of_topics` reads "Number of topics: must be between 2 and 200."
  - A failed analysis whose message is "ValueError: …" shows the generic sentence with Details in the Tasks panel and the tool tab.
  - Toasts with Details stay at least 15 s; the Error details dialog stays open after the toast closes.
  - Checked in WebKit by forcing a column rename failure.
- Implementation: backend `main.py`, `shared/errors.py` (`validation_message`, `_field_label`), `services/supervised_process.py` (`_user_message`, 5-tuple envelope), `services/analysis_execution.py`, `services/user_file_imports.py`; frontend `lib/apiError.ts`, `lib/errorPresentation.ts`, `lib/toastError.tsx`, `components/errors/ErrorDetails.tsx`, `components/errors/ErrorNotice.tsx`, `stores/errorDetailsStore.ts`, `components/layout/GlobalHosts.tsx`, `SidebarTasksSection.tsx`, `features/bootstrap/DataRootSetupForm.tsx`, plus call sites.
- Tests: backend `tests/unit/test_error_diagnostics.py` (validation message), `tests/unit/test_supervised_process.py` (user message passes through), `tests/integration/test_app_factory_runtime.py`, `test_workspace_resource_api.py`, `test_data_root_bootstrap.py`; frontend `lib/__tests__/apiError.test.ts`, `lib/__tests__/errorPresentation.test.ts`, `components/errors/__tests__/ErrorDetails.test.tsx`, `BackendBootstrapGate.test.tsx`, `useColumnMutations.test.tsx`, `generatedClientConfig.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: keep the API contract: `message` is for users; `details.diagnostic` holds the exception text; request id is shown only under Details as "Reference". Keep one shared Details dialog for toasts. DuckDB errors (for example `Binder Error: …`, `Conversion Error: …`) do not end in "Error:" after a class name in the same way as Python exceptions; the diagnostic-detection regex must be adapted or the backend must always separate diagnostic from message.

#### V07-108 AI provider failures and failed runs in plain words, with the diagnostic kept (#205 item 17)

- Commits: 35a73d7e
- Area: backend failure model (analyses, imports), Annotation providers; frontend Tasks panel, Concordance, Topic Modelling, Annotation Run
- Type: bug fix / wording (API model change)
- Problem / reason: provider failures were already classified (auth failed, rate limited, context too long, unavailable) but users saw raw SDK text such as "AuthenticationError: Error code: 401 - {...}".

Used for analysis admission (`analysis_start_failed`), execution (`analysis_execution_failed`), progress (`progress_invalid`) and user file imports (`user_file_import_execution_failed`).

    - `annotation_provider_rate_limited`: "{name} is receiving too many requests. Wait a minute and try again, or send fewer rows at a time."
    - `annotation_provider_request_rejected`: "{name} rejected the request. Check the model and settings, then try again."
    - `annotation_provider_unavailable`: "{name} is not responding. Check your internet connection or the provider's status, then try again."
    - `annotation_provider_context_limit`: "The text is too long for this model. Choose a model that takes longer texts, use fewer examples, or annotate shorter texts."
    - `annotation_provider_invalid_response`: "{name} returned an answer Wordflow couldn't read. Try again, or choose another model."
    - other: "The request to {name or 'the AI provider'} failed. Try again."
  - Model lists and Preview (`services/annotations.py`, `services/analysis_results.py`) raise with this message; the SDK text is the exception cause (so it appears in `details.diagnostic`). Failed Annotation Run rows (`workers/annotation.py`) store `message` = provider sentence and `diagnostic` = SDK text.
  - Interrupted import message "User File import was interrupted" to "The file import was interrupted. Import the files again."
  - Frontend: `presentError` accepts a stored failure object (`message` + `diagnostic`); `taskProjection` adds `error_detail` from `resource.error.diagnostic`; Tasks panel, Concordance, Topic Modelling and the Annotation Run show the diagnostic under Details (`ErrorNotice`, fallback "The Annotation Run failed. Try again.").
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A wrong OpenAI key gives "OpenAI rejected the API key. Check it in Settings." with the SDK text under Details.
  - A failed analysis whose exception is not user-written shows "Something went wrong in Wordflow. Try again. If it keeps happening, please send feedback with the details." and its diagnostic under Details in the Tasks panel and the tab.
  - The stored failure JSON of an analysis has `message`, `code`, and optionally `diagnostic`.
- Implementation: backend `domain/annotation.py`, `domain/background.py`, `models/analysis_results.py`, `services/failures.py` (new), `services/analyses.py`, `services/analysis_execution.py` (`_fail`), `services/analysis_results.py`, `services/annotations.py`, `services/user_file_imports.py`, `workers/annotation.py`; frontend `lib/errorPresentation.ts`, `task-stream/taskProjection.ts`, `SidebarTasksSection.tsx`, `AnnotationFeature.tsx`, `useAnalysisFeature.ts`, `ConcordanceFeature.tsx`, `TopicModelingFeature.tsx`, `TopicModelingResultsPanel.tsx`; `frontend/openapi/ldaca-wordflow.openapi.json`, `src/api/generated/types.gen.ts`.

#### V07-269 Slow runs show real step progress in Tasks, and that they are alive (#350)

- Commits: 930d4960, c4c03c7 (polars-text 0.6.5)
- Area: Errors and messages
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Chao, 2026-10-08: on a 2-vCPU Windows VM, Topic Modelling of 108,022 Reddit comments took several hours with the bar at 10% ("Grouping the text into topics") nearly all the time, worse than no bar. The whole polars-text call (segmenting, embedding, PaCMAP, HDBSCAN, topic words) ran between the 10% and 85% reports, and a Polars plugin cannot call back into Python. Chao: the same for Quotation and for tokenising large corpora; detail in Tasks, the analysis page only hints.
- What changed:
  - polars-text 0.6.5: `progress_path` on topic_modeling and tokenize writes `{step, steps, label, done, total, unit, updated_at_ms}` atomically, at most twice a second; tokenize chunks share one counter.
  - Progress gains optional `detail` (step, steps, step_label, done, total, unit, eta_seconds, processors_busy, processors, stalled_seconds). `workers/progress.StepReporter` turns counts into a monotonic fraction with bands per step, rate-limited reports, heartbeats, the measured ETA and the worker process's CPU use; `polars_text_progress` polls the file.
  - Topic Modelling: 5 steps (segmenting, reading the text into the model, arranging, grouping, topic words). Quotation: documents per document (local) or per batch (remote). Tokenising in Frequency (a band slice per Data Block) and Concordance tokens mode. LDaCA item import: items read.
  - UI: the analysis page shows one calm line, "Step 2 of 5: Reading the text into the model. Live progress in Tasks", which opens the Tasks section and expands the task; the task row shows counts, time left, "Working: using N of M processors", and a may-be-stuck note (10 min without progress on idle processors, or 2 min without a report).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A long Topic Modelling run shows which step it is on and, while embedding, "41,181 of 281,242 segments, about 5 min left" in Tasks; the analysis page line changes only between steps.
- Implementation: polars-text `src/progress.rs`; `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/workers/progress.py`, `domain/background.py`, the Topic Modelling, Quotation, Frequency, Concordance and LDaCA workers; frontend `RunStepNotice.tsx`, `TaskLiveness.tsx`, `taskFocusStore.ts`, `SidebarTasksSection.tsx`, `AnalysisRunningStateCard.tsx`, `AnalysisTaskBanner.tsx`.
- Tests: `test_worker_progress.py`, polars-text `test_progress.py` and Rust progress tests, `RunStepNotice.test.tsx`; live API and WebKit checks on the test instance.
- v0.8 notes: Same steps, counts, ETA and liveness for slow runs; a Rust backend can pass a callback instead of the file. Show detail in Tasks, a calm step line on the page.

#### V07-048 Row Details: remount the document box per row so Safari repaints highlights (#162)

- Commits: 1c40571f
- Area: Row Details (Quotation and all views using RowDetailPanel)
- Type: bug fix (Safari)
- Problem / reason: Flipping Quotation rows with Next/Previous in Safari often lost SPEAKER/VERB/QUOTE highlights (about 3 in 10 flips), although the DOM had them: Safari reused stale tiles when the scrolled box was updated in place and scrolled in the same frame.

#### V07-089 Analysis tools: parameters/results split with drag handle; results fill the pane; per-result resize grips (#196)

- Commits: 5923d34c, dc822b9b, b8b5dc73, 795b7f9a
- Area: Frequency, Concordance, Trends, Topic Modelling, Quotation, Annotation (layout)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: The tools stacked the parameters card and results in one scrolling column with no way to change how much height each part gets (the Data Loader already had a drag handle).
- What changed:
  - Split (5923d34c): parameters on top, results filling the rest, each scrolling on its own. A horizontal handle between them (`role="separator"`, `aria-label="Resize parameters and results"`, title "Drag to resize. Double-click to reset.") appears only once the results pane has content (MutationObserver on the pane). Drag sets the parameters height; ArrowUp/ArrowDown nudge by 40 px when focused; double-click returns to the natural height. Minimum parameters pane 96 px, minimum results pane 160 px. The default keeps parameters at natural height (as before). Each tool remembers its height in localStorage `ldaca.layout.analysisParametersHeight.<viewId>` (viewIds: `token-frequency`, `concordance`, `sequential-analysis`, `topic-modeling`, `quotation`, `annotation`). The tab host content wrapper became a bounded flex column (`AnalysisTabbedPanel`, ScrollArea replaced by a div).
  - Fill (dc822b9b): each tool's main result fills the results pane's spare height; results side by side (vertical extents overlap) share one row's share; stacked results split the slack. Each result stays within [min, pane height]; tables/lists (`fitContent`) stop at their full content height; below a result's minimum the pane scrolls. Covered: Concordance table (`concordance.table`), Quotation table (`quotation.table`), Concordance dispersion chart (`concordance.dispersion-chart`, min 180), Trends chart (`trends.chart`, min 240), Frequency list (`token-frequency.list`), Frequency word clouds (`token-frequency.cloud`, `token-frequency.unified-cloud`; `fill={false}`, keep their width-based height until resized), Topic Modelling bubble chart (`topic-modeling.bubbles`) and topic lists (`topic-modeling.selected-topics`, `topic-modeling.all-topics`, min 120), Annotation tables (`annotation.table`, shared by Manual, Preview and Review). Table frames default min 200.
  - A user size is remembered per result in localStorage `ldaca.layout.resultHeight.<storageKey>` (clamped to [min, 2400]) and takes the result out of the sharing; double-click on the grip returns it to filling.
  - Annotation's old bottom drag handle and per-tab `annotationTableHeight` setting (384 px default, max 75% of window, stored in the tab's annotation settings) are removed and replaced by the shared frame; old saved heights are ignored.
  - Own grip (b8b5dc73): dc822b9b first used the browser's native resize corner, which worked on tables but not on plots (chart canvas, word cloud, React Flow pane covered the corner). Each frame now draws its own 16 px grip at bottom-right above the content (`z-30`, classes `nodrag nopan nowheel`, `aria-label="Resize this result"`, title "Drag to resize. Double-click to fit the space again."), draggable with pointer capture, ArrowUp/Down nudging 40 px when focused, double-click to fit again.
  - Recalculate on rows (795b7f9a): a `fitContent` frame that got its height before its rows loaded stayed at minimum (Quotation opened about 120 px tall). The frame now observes its scrolled content (`[data-result-frame-scroll]` or the ScrollArea viewport's first child) with a ResizeObserver and asks the pane to recalculate.
  - ui.md and annotation.md updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Before any results, no handle; after a Concordance preview, the handle appears.
  - Dragging the handle down grows the parameters pane and shrinks the results, down to 160 px results.
  - Reloading keeps the dragged height per tool; double-click resets.
  - With a Concordance table, dragging the handle up makes the table taller (more rows visible) until its full height.
  - Dragging a result's grip sets its height (e.g. word cloud 472 to 402 px); reload keeps it; double-click returns it to filling.
  - Quotation results open at a filled height, not about 120 px.
  - Two side-by-side word clouds or lists share one row height.
- Implementation: new `frontend/src/features/views/common/components/AnalysisSplitLayout.tsx`, `ResultFrame.tsx` (`ResultFrame`, `ResultChartFit` subtracting toolbar/legend height for ECharts), `analysisResultsFill.ts` (`ResultsFillContext`, `useResultsFillEngine` computing slack from pane inner height minus content height with rAF scheduling, `useResultsFill`); `AnalysisTableScrollArea.tsx` (`AnalysisTableFrame` `resultKey` prop, grid rows `minmax(0,1fr)_auto`); `ResponsiveWordCloud.tsx` (`height` prop); each tool feature file (`TokenFrequencyFeature`, `ConcordanceFeature`, `SequentialAnalysisFeature`, `TopicModelingFeature`, `QuotationFeature`, `AnnotationFeature`) and result components (`SequentialChart`, `TokenFrequencySingleTokenSection`, `TokenFrequencyUnifiedTokenSection`, `TopicModelingBubbleChartSection`, `TopicSelectionPanel`, `ConcordanceDispersionSummary`, `AnnotationTableFrame`); removed `annotation/annotationTableHeight.ts`.
- Tests: `AnalysisSplitLayout.test.tsx` (5 cases: handle only with content, natural height default, drag and remember and double-click reset, restore and arrow keys, minimum), `ResultFrame.test.tsx` (default height outside a pane, natural height, restores user size, double-click forgets, drag and arrow keys), `AnnotationTableFrame.test.tsx` ("offers a corner resize grip instead of the old bottom handle", "ignores a table height saved by earlier versions"), `useAnnotationTabSettings.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: Sizes are per browser (localStorage), not per project. The own-grip technique is needed wherever a canvas covers the corner (WebKit and all browsers). A later follow-up 9dfd2e85 (outside this range) put Topic Modelling's Selected and All Topics lists in one frame. Checked in WebKit for Concordance and Frequency; Topic Modelling, Trends, Quotation and Annotation were not run in the browser at dc822b9b.

#### V07-105 Tables keep their scroll position when sorted, paged or updated (#209)

- Commits: 0f3ab6e6, 9d070cd7 (builds on d8e7790d from #208)
- Area: all result tables: Data Editor, Concordance, Quotation, Frequency Keyword Analysis statistics, Data Builder preview; pagination footer
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: tables and results panes jumped to the top (or top-left) after sorting, paging or a column change, most visibly in Safari/WebKit (used by the desktop app). Three separate causes.
- What changed:
  1. Cells remounting (`flexRender`). TanStack's `flexRender` mounts each function header/cell renderer as a React component; column definitions are rebuilt every render, so every header and cell remounted (losing tooltips and focus, and WebKit reset the scroll). Fix: shared `renderColumnPart(part, context)` calls the renderer as a function. Used by all tables. ESLint `no-restricted-imports` now errors on importing `flexRender` from `@tanstack/react-table` with the message "Use renderColumnPart from @/lib/table/renderColumnPart: flexRender remounts cells and resets table scrolling."
  2. Rows swapped for a loading row. While a page or sort loaded, Concordance and Quotation replaced all rows with one spinner row, so the table shrank and the browser clamped the scroll to the top. Fix: rows stay in place, dimmed and not clickable (`busyTableBodyProps(loading && rows.length > 0)` gives `aria-busy`, `pointer-events-none opacity-60`); `PaginatedTableProcessingRow` shows only when there are no rows yet. The footer spinner now has `role="status"` and `aria-label="Loading page"`.
  3. WebKit lays out mid-update and clamps the pane. With the results pane scrolled to the Keyword table, sorting LL moved it 557 to 357 px, Next page 557 to 277 px. No script scrolled it (no `scrollTop`/`scrollTo`/`scrollIntoView`, no focus change, WebKit 26 has no `overflow-anchor`). React removes the old rows before inserting the new ones in one commit, and WebKit can run layout in between (for example after header sort icons swap), so the content is briefly shorter and the pane's scroll is clamped. Fix: `useStableTableHeight()` returns a ref for a `div` wrapping the `<table>`; in a `useLayoutEffect` after every render it sets the wrapper's `min-height` to the table's current height (`Math.ceil(getBoundingClientRect().height)`), so the next update cannot shrink the wrapper below the previous height, and a real height change applies after that update. Wraps Keyword Analysis, Data Editor, Concordance, Quotation and Data Builder preview tables. The wrapper must go inside any `overflow-x-auto` box, not on it (otherwise the horizontal scrollbar can add a vertical one).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Sorting a column in any of the five tables keeps both the table's own scroll position and the surrounding results pane position; new rows appear in place.
  - Changing a column in the Data Editor (type, rename, a tool edit) keeps the scroll position.
  - Paging moves the table to its first row on purpose but keeps the horizontal position; the results pane does not move.
  - While a sort or page loads, current rows stay visible, dimmed and not clickable, and the footer spinner is announced "Loading page"; a spinner row appears only before any rows exist.
  - Before/after in WebKit: Keyword LL sort 557 to 357 px (before) and holds at 557 (after); Next page 557 to 277 (before), holds (after); Concordance sort (150, 250) to (150, 0) (before), holds (after); Data Editor type change (400, 150) to (0, 0) (before), holds (after); Quotation sort with 419 rows holds at (0, 250).
- Implementation: `frontend/src/lib/table/renderColumnPart.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/table/useStableTableHeight.ts`, `frontend/src/features/views/common/components/paginatedTableBusy.ts`, `ServerPaginationFooter.tsx`, `ConcordanceRowsTable.tsx`, `QuotationNodeBlock.tsx`, `TokenFrequencyStatisticsTable.tsx`, `preprocessing/components/PreviewTable.tsx`, `WorkspaceTable.tsx`, `frontend/eslint.config.js`.
- Tests: `frontend/src/lib/table/__tests__/useStableTableHeight.test.tsx` (min-height 280px for 10 rows at 28 px, then 112px for 4 rows); `ConcordanceTableNodeBlock.test.tsx` and `QuotationNodeBlock.test.tsx` changed from "stale rows hidden while loading" to "rows stay, dimmed and busy".
- v0.8 notes (rules): column renderers must be plain functions without hooks (put hooks in a component the renderer returns), never mounted per render; keep rows while loading instead of swapping to a single spinner row; any table whose rows are replaced inside a scrolling pane needs a stable-height wrapper or equivalent. Verify in WebKit/Safari: long Frequency pane with two corpora, sort several columns and page, the pane must not move; in the Data Editor and Concordance scroll right and down, then sort; Data Editor type change must hold position.

#### V07-194 What you see is what you get: Add to Project and table exports follow the table's sort (#275)

- Commits: b82beae8
- Area: Tables and pagination
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Decided by Chao on 2026-10-03: a Data Block added from a result table, and a table export, should have its rows in the order the table shows, as Trends and Dispersion already keep their selections and legend exclusions. Topic Modelling is out of scope (its topic list is not user-sortable). Mapping the paths also found that the unsortable Dispersion documents table received the Table view's sort: after an L1 sort its query was refused, and after a metadata sort the documents were silently reordered.
- What changed:
  - `SortedDataBlockCreationSource` (`domain/workspace/analysis.py`) adds `sort_by` (default `None`, Data Block order) and `descending`; used by Concordance match and Quotation Add to Project. Older stored requests still validate.
  - `analysis/result_sort.py::sort_result_rows` is the one sort for the Review page (`_projected_artifact_page`) and the creation worker (`_sort_like_the_table` in `workers/result_data_block_creation.py`): chosen column, then source row and match position, always ascending (#266); case folded for L1, R1 and matched text with Case sensitive off (#267, #274). Preparation forwards each Concordance source's `case_sensitive`. The worker refuses columns the table cannot sort (document, metadata and Concordance's scalar `CONC_` fields only).
  - Frontend: Concordance sends each source's Review sort (`concordanceDetachSort`: Combined view or an empty sort mean none); Quotation sends `runAllReviewQuery`'s sort; Dispersion Add Documents unchanged (its table is unsortable, in Data Block order).
  - Keyword Analysis CSV uses TanStack's sorted row model, which already comes after the token filter (it used the filtered model, before sorting, while a filter was on).
  - The Dispersion documents query is always unsorted; the Table view keeps its sort for when the user switches back.
  - Help: `concordance.md`, `quotation.md`, `token-frequency.md`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Sort a Concordance source's table by L1 descending (Case sensitive off), Add to Project: the Data Block's rows are in that order, "The" and "the" together in Data Block order. Unsorted or Combined view: Data Block order.
  - Sort a Quotation result by a metadata column, Add to Project: quotes in that order.
  - Keyword Analysis with a token filter and a sort: the CSV has the filtered rows in the sorted order.
  - Sort Table view by L1, switch to Dispersion: the documents table loads in Data Block order (no error); back in Table view the L1 sort is still applied.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/analysis/result_sort.py`, `services/analysis_results.py`, `services/analysis_preparation_registry.py`, `workers/result_data_block_creation.py`, `workers/invocations.py`, `workers/entrypoints.py`, `domain/workspace/analysis.py`; `frontend/src/features/views/concordance/ConcordanceFeature.tsx`, `concordanceTablePresentation.ts`, `hooks/useConcordanceResultSession.ts`; `quotation/QuotationFeature.tsx`; `common/components/ResultAddToWorkspaceDialog.tsx`; `token-frequency/components/results/TokenFrequencyStatisticsTable.tsx`.
- Tests: `test_result_data_block_creation_worker.py` (`test_match_data_block_creation_keeps_the_tables_sort`, `test_match_data_block_creation_refuses_a_column_the_table_cannot_sort`, `test_quotation_data_block_creation_keeps_the_tables_sort`); `concordanceTablePresentation.test.ts` (`concordanceDetachSort`); `useConcordanceResultSession.test.tsx` ("never sends the Table sort to the unsortable Dispersion documents"); `TokenFrequencyResultsLayout.test.tsx` ("exports filtered Keyword Analysis rows in the order the table shows").
- v0.8 notes: every detach and export from a sortable table must use the same sort code as the table page (one helper), including tie keys and case rules; never re-implement the order separately.

#### V07-237 Empty values sort last in every table, in both directions (#317)

- Commits: 2d4a0ace
- Area: Tables and pagination
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Chao, 2026-10-06: Chao and Alex agreed for v0.8 that empty metadata values sort last in the Concordance table, and the same rule applies to every table sort. v0.7 sorted nulls first in both directions (Polars default; the Data View SQL said NULLS FIRST), and NaN and blank text followed neither rule. This replaces the "empty values come first in either direction" behaviour recorded in V07-062 and V07-213.
- What changed:
  - New `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/shared/empty_values.py`: `empty_value_expression(column, dtype)` (moved from `node_operations.py`; missing, NaN for floats, blank or whitespace-only text for String/Categorical/Enum) and `empty_last_key(column, dtype, key=None)`, which turns empty values into null; callers sort with `nulls_last=True`. The dtype must be the schema's instance (an `isinstance` check on Categorical).
  - Applied to: Concordance Preview in text and Tokens mode (`concordance_core.py`, `concordance_tokens.py`), Quotation Preview (`quotation_core.py`), Concordance/Quotation Review match tables and Add to Project (`result_sort.sort_result_rows`, case-folded key kept, emptiness judged on the column), Concordance Review documents (`_concordance_document_projection_page`), the JSON result sort (`_sort_rows`: NaN and blank text now count as empty), the paging helper (`table_transport.materialize_page`) and the Data Editor sort derivation (`nulls_last=True`).
  - Data View: `sqlOrder(column, descending, empty)` in `frontend/src/api/workspaceSql.ts` emits `CASE WHEN <empty> THEN NULL ELSE col END ASC|DESC NULLS LAST`; `<empty>` is `TRIM(CAST(col AS VARCHAR)) = ''` for text and category columns and `col = CAST('NaN' AS DOUBLE)` for floats (Polars SQL has no isnan; it treats NaN as equal to NaN). The Data View stores the kind with the sort request (`sort_empty`), taken from the column's Arrow field at click time.
  - Keyness statistics table (client-side TanStack v9): N/A values (NaN) become `undefined` sort keys, with `sortUndefined: 'last'` on every numeric column; Signed LL sorts by a new `sort_signed_ll`. CSV export is unchanged (it skips `sort_*` fields).
  - Help: Concordance ("Empty values (missing or blank) come last in either direction"), Interface ("empty cells stay at the bottom either way"), Frequency ("N/A values stay at the bottom whichever way you sort"); ADR 0018.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Data View, text column ['b', missing, ' ', 'a', 'Zed']: ascending Zed, a, b then the two empty cells; descending b, a, Zed then the empty cells (Playwright WebKit).
  - Data View, number column [1.5, missing, NaN, 0.5, 2]: 0.5, 1.5, 2 then missing and NaN; descending 2, 1.5, 0.5 then the same two.
  - Concordance Preview sorted by a metadata column puts rows with missing or blank values last in both directions; Review ties among empty rows keep reading order.
  - Keyness: %DIFF N/A rows (no Reference hits) stay at the bottom when sorting %DIFF either way.
- Implementation: `shared/empty_values.py`, `shared/table_transport.py`, `services/node_operations.py`, `services/analysis_results.py`, `analysis/result_sort.py`, `analysis/quotation_core.py`, `analysis/concordance_core.py`, `analysis/concordance_tokens.py`; `frontend/src/api/workspaceSql.ts`, `data-view/hooks/useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`, `token-frequency/components/results/TokenFrequencyStatisticsTable.tsx`.
- Tests: `test_empty_last_sorting.py` (key on text, category and float in both directions; result rows; paging helper; Concordance Preview; JSON results); `workspaceSql.test.ts`; `TokenFrequencyResultsLayout.test.tsx` ("keeps N/A statistics last", fails on the old code); Playwright WebKit Data View check.
- v0.8 notes: This is the agreed v0.8 rule: every ORDER BY on a user-chosen column uses an empty-aware key and NULLS LAST in both directions (DuckDB's default is NULLS LAST only for ASC). Empty must include NaN (`isnan`) and blank text (`trim(x) = ''`), not only NULL. Client-side sorts must keep N/A last too.

### Renaming and tabs

#### V07-045 Tab strips: stop over-scrolling in Safari; show more-tabs indicators (#157)

- Commits: 71845ef7
- Area: Tabs (all EditorTabs strips: Data Builder tools, analysis tabs, etc.)
- Type: bug fix (Safari) / feature
- Problem / reason: WebKit counted the absolutely positioned, translated tabs as extra overflow: scrollWidth 2404 for 851px of tabs (Chromium 859), so the Data Builder tabs scrolled far past the last tab.
- What changed: The tab row is wrapped in a `relative overflow-hidden` div sized to the computed row width (tabs + create button). When tabs are hidden past an edge, that edge fades (32px mask gradient) and a small chevron button appears there ("Show earlier tabs" / "Show more tabs") that scrolls by max(80% of the strip width, TAB_MIN_WIDTH). The buttons sit outside the `role="tablist"` element, so keyboard order is unchanged.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - In WebKit the scroll range beyond the tabs equals the hidden part (about 313px, Chromium 321px) instead of about 1866px.
  - Indicators appear only on the side with hidden tabs; one click reaches the end when little is hidden.
  - Arrow-key navigation between tabs is unchanged.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/components/tabs/EditorTabs.tsx` (`edgeMask`, `scrollStep`, `EdgeScrollButton`, `hiddenEdges` state updated on scroll and ResizeObserver).
- Tests: `components/tabs/__tests__/EditorTabs.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: WebKit-specific overflow behaviour with transformed absolutely positioned children; clip to a computed width in any custom tab strip and verify in WebKit.

#### V07-095 Active tab marked by an accent bar (#202)

- Commits: b2201139
- Area: shared tabs (analysis tab strip `EditorTabs`, segmented tabs `ui/tabs.tsx` used by Data Builder tools, Table/Dispersion, Cloud/List and so on)
- Type: behaviour change (visual accessibility)
- Problem / reason: the active tab differed from the others only by its fill, which in the light theme is a 60% transparent grey (`#DADADA99`). On low-contrast screens the active tab could not be told apart.
- What changed:
  - Analysis tab strip: the active tab text is semibold and a 2 px rounded bar in the theme focus colour is drawn under the text (inset 6 px left/right, 4 px from the bottom).
  - Segmented tabs: the active trigger gets an inset bottom shadow of 2 px in the same colour (`shadow-[inset_0_-2px_0_var(--color-tab-active-indicator)]`), keeps its semibold weight; inactive triggers use the muted/description text colour and turn to foreground on hover.
  - New CSS token `--color-tab-active-indicator: var(--vscode-focusBorder)` in `index.css`, so light and dark themes stay consistent.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - In any tab strip, exactly one tab (the active one) shows a 2 px accent bar at its bottom edge in the focus-border colour.
  - The active tab label is semibold; inactive segmented tab labels are in the muted colour.
  - Works in both light and dark themes.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/components/tabs/EditorTabs.tsx` (span `data-testid="editor-tab-active-indicator"`, `aria-hidden`, `pointer-events-none`), `frontend/src/components/ui/tabs.tsx` (`TabsTrigger` classes), `frontend/src/index.css` (token).
- Tests: none added.
- v0.8 notes: keep an indicator that does not rely on fill colour alone (a bar or underline plus weight). Use a theme token rather than a hard-coded colour. Verified in WebKit on the analysis strip and Data Builder tabs.

#### V07-128 One rule for failed renames, shared by every rename box (#210)

- Commits: 124ac790
- Area: Data Editor column rename, shared tab strip (Data Editor tabs and analysis tabs), Project name in the graph header, Data Block rename dialog (graph card and sidebar)
- Type: bug fix / behaviour change
- Problem / reason: after a failed rename (for example a column name that already exists) the box stayed open and clicking elsewhere submitted the same name again, raising a second error. Also Data Block and tab rename failures were silent because the handlers did not return their promise.
- What changed:
  - New hook `useInlineRename({ original, onSubmit, onClose, validate?, failureTitle? = "Couldn't rename." })` returning `{ inputProps, pending, submit, cancel }`. Rules:
    - Enter submits, and always tries again after a failure; Esc cancels (and stops propagation).
    - Empty text: toast "Enter a name." and stays open. Text equal to the original (trimmed): closes without a request. `validate` message: toast and stays open.
    - `onSubmit` rejects: `toastError(error, 'Try again.', { title: failureTitle })`, box stays open, focus returns and the text is selected (via `requestAnimationFrame`). `onSubmit` resolves `false`: caller showed its own message; box stays open.
    - Blur with text unchanged since the failure: closes and keeps the original name, no second request. Blur with edited text: submits. Blur while a request is pending: ignored. Input is `readOnly` while pending and `aria-invalid` after a failure.
  - Used by: `RenameInput` (Data Editor columns; `submitRename` now resolves true/false, its own toasts on failure), `EditorTabs` (`TabRenameInput`, aria "Rename tab"; `onRename` may return a promise; `useWorkspaceTabs.renameTab` uses `mutateAsync`), Project name (`WorkspaceControls` `ProjectNameInput`, failure title "Couldn't rename the Project."; its old "Invalid Project name" alert dialog with "Got it" is replaced by a toast).
  - `DataBlockRenameDialog`: on failure shows toast "Couldn't rename the Data Block." and stays open with the text selected; input read-only while pending. Sidebar and graph (`useWorkspaceGraph.handleRename`) now return the rename promise.
  - Help ui.md: "Every rename box (tabs, columns, the Project name, Data Blocks) works the same way. Enter saves and Esc cancels. Clicking elsewhere saves an edited name. If a rename fails, for example because the name is already used, a message explains why and the box stays open with the name selected so you can fix it. Press Enter to try again, or click elsewhere without changing it to keep the original name."
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Rename a column to an existing name: one request, a toast, box stays open with the text selected; click away: box closes, original name kept, no second request.
  - Same for a Data Editor tab, an analysis tab and the Project name (checked in WebKit with forced server failures).
  - Edit the text after a failure, then click away: the new text is submitted.
  - Enter after a failure retries even with unchanged text.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/lib/rename/useInlineRename.ts` (new), `components/tabs/EditorTabs.tsx`, `components/tabs/editorTabsInteractionState.ts` (draft title removed from reducer state), `components/layout/WorkspaceControls.tsx`, `components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`, `components/layout/NodeActionsToolbar.tsx`, `features/workspace/common/components/DataBlockRenameDialog.tsx`, `data-view/components/RenameInput.tsx`, `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx`, `data-view/hooks/useColumnMutations.ts`, `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`, `graph-view/components/CustomNode.tsx`, `graph-view/hooks/useWorkspaceGraph.ts`, `views/common/tabs/useWorkspaceTabs.ts`, `AnalysisTabbedPanel.tsx`.
- Tests: `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/useInlineRename.test.tsx` (8 cases), `DataBlockRenameDialog.test.tsx`, `editorTabsInteractionState.test.ts`, `useWorkspaceTabs.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes (from the issue): Data Block names are DuckDB table names in v0.8, so clashes will be common. Keep the hook contract (`onSubmit` rejects or resolves `false` to keep the box open) and route every rename through it. Handlers must return their promise so failures reach the toast. See the regression note in the item 25 entry about `getInvalidWorkspaceNameMessage`.

#### V07-129 Analysis tabs named with the tool prefix: F-1, C-1, T-1, TM-1, Q-1, A-1 (#211)

- Commits: be7f320f
- Area: analysis tabs, Tasks panel, quick access, stop-word list sources
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: tabs were named 1, 2, 3, which looked thin and could not be told apart across tools.
- What changed:
  - New tabs are named `{prefix}-{n}`: Frequency "F", Concordance "C", Trends "T", Topic Modelling "TM", Quotation "Q", Annotation "A" (`tabPrefix` in `analysisNavigation.ts`). The first tab created automatically is no longer hard-coded "1".
  - `nextTabTitle(names, kind)`: next number is max(existing default numbers) + 1, counting legacy names; if none are default names, `names.length + 1`. So after C-9 comes C-10.
  - `displayTabTitle(name, kind)`: a stored bare number ("3") or legacy "Analysis 3" displays as "F-3" (for that tool's prefix); a name matching `{PREFIX}-{n}` for the same tool is a default name; anything else (renamed tabs) is shown as is. Stored names are not rewritten.
  - Tasks panel rows use the tab's own name (was "{short tool} - {tab}", for example "Freq - 1"): "F-1", "C-2 · Run", a renamed tab "JP vs AUS". Quick access reads "Frequency: F-1". Stop-word list sources read "{tool} · F-1".
  - Builder (B-1) not applied: the Builder has sub-tabs, not analysis tabs.
  - Help ui.md updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Opening Concordance in a new Project creates tab "C-1"; adding another gives "C-2".
  - A Project saved with a Frequency tab named "3" shows it as "F-3"; the next new Frequency tab is "F-4".
  - A renamed tab "JP vs AUS" keeps its name in the tab, Tasks panel and quick access.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/features/views/common/analysisNavigation.ts` (`tabPrefix`, `defaultTabNumber`, `displayTabTitle`, `nextTabTitle`, `LEGACY_DEFAULT_TAB_TITLE = /^(?:Analysis )?(\d+)$/`), `tabs/AnalysisTabsHost.tsx`, `tabs/tabStateOps.ts`, `tabs/useWorkspaceTabs.ts`, `components/layout/sidebar/taskRows.ts`, `common/utils/stopWordListSources.ts`.
- Tests: `analysisNavigation.test.ts`, `SidebarTasksSection.test.tsx`, `AnalysisTabsHost.test.tsx`, `DesktopNavigationHeader*.test.tsx`, `stopWordListSources.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: v0.8 must read Projects whose tab names are "N", "Analysis N" or "{PREFIX}-N" and display them consistently. A user who deliberately renames a tab to exactly "3" will see it shown as "F-3" (limitation of the display mapping).

#### V07-247 Tab strips draw their own slim scroll bar

- Commits: 69ebb287
- Area: Renaming and tabs
- Type: behaviour change (visual)
- Problem / reason: Chao, 2026-10-07: the bar under the tab strips (Data Builder tools, analysis tabs) looked clunky, but it is needed by people without horizontal scrolling (no trackpad or tilt wheel). Native bars draw differently per browser (Chromium ignores custom styling once scrollbar-width is set), so tab strips hide theirs and draw their own.
- What changed:
  - New `components/tabs/StripScrollBar.tsx`: a 2px faint rail inset to the tabs and a 3px thumb at 45% strength that grows and darkens while pointed at, shown only while the tabs overflow; drag the thumb, or click the rail to move there. `railWidth = Math.max(1, clientWidth - 16)` is computed from tracked metrics because refs may not be read during render.
  - `EditorTabs.tsx` tracks `scrollMetrics` (scrollLeft, scrollWidth, clientWidth) on scroll and resize; `index.css` hides the native bar of `.editor-tabs-scroll` (`scrollbar-width: none`, `::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none }`).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - With more tabs than fit, a slim bar appears under the strip; dragging its thumb scrolls the tabs; clicking the rail jumps there (Playwright WebKit).
  - With few tabs, no bar is drawn.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/components/tabs/StripScrollBar.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/tabs/EditorTabs.tsx`, `frontend/src/index.css`.
- Tests: `StripScrollBar.test.tsx`; Playwright WebKit (drag and click).
- v0.8 notes: Keep a visible, drawn scroll affordance under overflowing tab strips; do not rely on native scroll bars.

### Wording across the app

#### V07-018 "Workspace" renamed to "Project" in all user-visible text (#132)

- Commits: 12970c61
- Area: UI-wide wording (Data Loader, Project manager, Settings, analysis dialogs, backend error messages, Docs)
- Type: wording
- Problem / reason: "Workspace" is not a familiar term for qualitative and HASS researchers.
- What changed: About 330 user-visible strings across about 100 frontend files (labels, buttons, dialogs, toasts, tooltips, aria labels, contextual hints, help-link labels), 17 help pages (prose only; code spans, anchors and image paths unchanged), and about 204 backend error messages that can reach the UI now say Project. Examples: "Project manager" (was "Workspace manager"), Settings tab "Project" (was "Workspace"), "Add to Project" (was "Add to Workspace"), "Project Graph View", "Favorite Projects", "No favorite projects saved.", "Active project", "Create project", "Rename project", "Project description", "Project name", "Unload project", "Invalid project name", "Load a Project to access analysis Tabs.", "This Project has no analysis Tabs.", "Could not load Project Tabs.", "Load a project to use this view", tooltip "Choose the folder where Wordflow will keep projects, imports, and application data."; backend "Project not found", "Project is not open", "Project is closing", "Project revision is stale", "Project snapshot exceeds its limit", "Project data could not be persisted", "Invalid project name: <reason>". Note casing is inconsistent in places (for example header fallback "No Project" vs tabs placeholder "No project").
  - Unchanged: code identifiers (e.g. `AddToWorkspaceDialog`, `useWorkspaceTabs`), API routes (`/api/workspaces`), `workspace.json`, archive formats, query/mutation keys, CSS container names, developer docs. The invalid-name matcher (`getInvalidWorkspaceNameMessage`) accepts both "Invalid workspace name" and "Invalid project name" for older backends.
  - Help screenshots still show "Workspace" (retaken later under #119, outside this range).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - No user-visible string in the UI contains "Workspace" (except possibly screenshots); analysis dialogs say "Add to Project".
  - Backend errors shown in toasts say "Project".
  - Existing saved projects and archives still load.
- Implementation: many files; notable `components/dialogs/SettingsDialog.tsx`, `components/layout/{DesktopNavigationHeader,Sidebar,WorkspaceControls,WorkspaceShell,BackendBootstrapGate}.tsx`, `features/guidance/registry.ts`, `features/views/data-loader/components/ActiveWorkspaceCard.tsx`, `features/views/common/components/AddToWorkspaceDialog.tsx`, backend `services/workspace.py`, `services/workspace_archives.py`, `infrastructure/storage/workspace_store.py`, `domain/workspace/graph.py`, `api/workspaces/archives.py`.
- Tests: many frontend tests updated for new strings (e.g. `DataLoaderFeature.test.tsx`, `DesktopNavigationHeader.test.tsx`, `ResultAddToWorkspaceDialog.test.tsx`, `ConcordanceFeature.test.tsx`), `backend/tests/unit/test_workspace_store.py`.
- v0.8 notes: v0.8 UI should use "Project" for what the code calls a workspace. Keep the internal names only if v0.8 needs archive compatibility.

#### V07-059 Wording: replace "materialized" in docs and import errors (#173)

- Commits: 4a68e7eb
- Area: Docs, Errors (project import), Concordance docs
- Type: wording
- Problem / reason: User feedback: "materialize" was used where "process all" was meant; use plain terms.
- What changed: Concordance help uses "processed"/"loaded" (e.g. "Preview has not processed the ...", "loading a whole-corpus result into the browser", troubleshooting row "Sorting generated columns needs every match, which only Run All processes"). Import errors: "This project file could not be imported safely. Export the project again and retry." (two places in `services/workspace.py`) and "This project file contains data that could not be read. Export the project again and retry." (`services/workspace_archives.py`).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - No user-facing "materialized" in Concordance docs or import errors.
- Implementation: files listed above plus `information/concordance.md`, `tutorials/concordance.md`.
- Tests: none.
- v0.8 notes: Keep plain-language error messages that tell the user what to do.

#### V07-100 UI wording and layout nits from the docs pass (#201)

- Commits: 19124b37, 7cae68a5, bc473142
- Area: Annotation, Data Loader sample data, pagination footer, Concordance/Quotation results, Data Builder (Stack, Filter, Group, Deduplicate), docs
- Type: wording + layout fix
- Problem / reason: found while taking v0.7 help screenshots: "Example Node", old tool names in sample data tags, "Rows per page" wrapping onto three lines in the Data Editor footer, Quotation and Concordance both titled "Search Results", Stack card titled "Concatenate Datasets".
- What changed:
  - Annotation AI panel: example inputs title "Example Node" to "Example Data Block" (the column button's accessible name follows: "Change Example Data Block text column").
  - Import sample data dialog tool tags (`TOOL_LABELS`): "Token Frequency" to "Frequency", "Sequential Analysis" to "Trends".
  - Result card titles: Concordance preview "Search Results" to "Concordance Results"; Quotation preview "Search Results" to "Quotation Results" (component defaults too). (The Run view title "Review" was replaced by the same titles in 4d85698e.)
  - Stack card title "Concatenate Datasets" to "Stack Data Blocks"; its help icon label "Concat sub-tab overview" to "About Stack". The option "Drop duplicate rows after stacking" moves from the card body to the footer row beside the create button and is renamed "Deduplicate", help label "About Deduplicate", tooltip "Keep one copy of rows that are the same in every column, as the Deduplicate tool does." (old tooltip: "Run polars .unique() across all columns so identical rows from different inputs collapse into one.").
  - New-block name labels: Filter no longer shows "Result: New Data Block" (the apply bar now shows text only in update mode: "Updates the selected Data Block", old "Result: Updates the selected Data Block"). Deduplicate label "Name the new data blocks" to "New data block names" with hint "Creates {base}_deduplicated and {base}_duplicates.". Group label "Name prefix" to "New data block names" with hint "Each block is named after this and its group, for example {groupBlockName(prefix, first ticked group)}." Default `nameLabel` stays "New data block name". New `nameHint` prop on `BuilderToolCard`.
  - Pagination footer (compact mode, used by the Data Editor): lays out by its own width with a CSS container query (`@container/pager`), not the viewport. Below 520 px of footer width, page size and page buttons stack on two rows, and the "Rows per page" label is `whitespace-nowrap`. First version used `28rem`, but the app's root font size is 13 px so 28rem was only 364 px and the ~386 px Data Editor column kept the one-row layout, cutting off Next; 7cae68a5 changed the breakpoint to `520px`.
  - Docs: preprocessing.md Stack described as "Put two or more data blocks that share the same columns one below the other" (no "concatenate"); Deduplicate option section renamed; Group text says "change the first part of the names under **New data block names**". bc473142 retook screenshots (Stack card and schema status, Filter name row, Group values and dates, Deduplicate, Data Builder and main window, Import sample data, Quotation results, Annotation AI settings).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Annotation AI settings show "Example Data Block".
  - Sample data dialog tags read "Frequency" and "Trends".
  - In a Data Editor column narrower than 520 px, the footer shows the page size on one row and the page buttons on a second row, with Next fully visible; "Rows per page" is on one line.
  - Concordance and Quotation result cards are titled "Concordance Results" and "Quotation Results".
  - Stack card is "Stack Data Blocks"; a "Deduplicate" checkbox sits next to the create button.
  - Filter's apply bar has no "Result:" text.
- Implementation: `AnnotationFeature.tsx`, `SampleDataPanel.tsx`, `common/components/ServerPaginationFooter.tsx`, `ConcordanceFeature.tsx`, `ConcordanceResultsPanel.tsx`, `QuotationFeature.tsx`, `QuotationResultsPanel.tsx`, `preprocessing/concat/ConcatSubTab.tsx`, `preprocessing/components/PreprocessingApplyBar.tsx`, `preprocessing/builder/BuilderToolCard.tsx`, `dedupe/DedupeSubTab.tsx`, `split-group/SplitByGroupSubTab.tsx`, `frontend/public/tutorials/preprocessing.md`.
- Tests: `AnnotationFeature.test.tsx` (new label), `DataPreprocessingFeature.test.tsx` (apply bar has "New data block name" and no "Result:").
- v0.8 notes: use container queries (component width), not viewport breakpoints, for footers inside resizable panes; remember the 13 px root font when converting rem breakpoints. Several of these labels were changed again the same day by 4d85698e (for example create buttons became "Add to Project").

#### V07-101 Plain, consistent terms across tools: agreed #205 review decisions (#205)

- Area: all analysis tools, Data Builder, sidebar, guidance hints, help pages, a few backend task messages
- Type: wording

Disabled reasons "Wait for Run All to finish" to "Wait for {runAllLabel} to finish" / "Wait for Run to finish", "Run All is already running" to "Run is already running". Sort hint "Run All to enable sorting" to "Run to enable sorting". Re-run reason "Clear Results before running again" to "Choose Clear before running again".

  - Tasks panel step label for `*_run_all` requests: "Run All" to "Run" (row example "Conc - 2 · Run"). Backend success messages: "Concordance Run All completed successfully" to "Concordance Run completed successfully"; same for Quotation.

(reworded again in item 16); provider delete text "Completed Run All output" to "Completed Run output".

Use …"; over-limit "Currently {count} selected." to "Currently {count} added."; Export empty message to "No data blocks added yet. Use Add data block, or Add All to add every Data Block."
