Slower, but often more accurate."; Custom "Let the model think step by step before it answers. Use only if your model supports it."
  - Temperature descriptions: OpenAI "Lower values give more consistent answers; higher values more varied ones. Not used while Thinking is on."; Google and OpenRouter "…Not every model uses it."; Custom "…Use only if your model supports it."
  - Run settings: "These controls belong to Wordflow and apply to every provider." to "These settings apply to every provider."; legend "Run processing" to "Which rows to annotate"; "Reprocess all rows" to "Annotate all rows again"; "Fill missing only" to "Only rows without an annotation"; explanation "Annotating all rows again replaces the annotation column; the other choice keeps the annotations you already have."; "Batch size" to "Rows per request", help "How many rows go to the AI model in each request (default 20, up to 100)."; "Max retries per batch" to "Retries if a request fails", help "How many more times to try a request that fails (default 2, so 3 tries in all). 0 means no retries."

### Data Loader and files

#### V07-019 Add a folder of texts as one Data Block; shared document rule for folders and ZIPs; skipped-files report (#134)

- Commits: 6b3fc68f, e6102316
- Area: Data Loader
- Type: feature + behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Folders could be uploaded recursively but not loaded, although a ZIP of the same texts could. ZIP loading made a row from every member that decoded as UTF-8, whatever the extension, so a metadata CSV became one document holding the whole table; non-UTF-8 files were skipped silently. The first version of the skip report was a separate toast that the success toast stacked on top of and hid.
- What changed:
  - Folder rows in the File Tree get a "+" icon button (aria-label "Add folder <name> as a Data Block"; title "Add the text files in this folder as one Data Block", or "Load a project to add this folder as a Data Block" when no project is loaded, then disabled). It opens the preview dialog titled "Add Folder: <name>" with description "Every .txt, .text, .md, .rst and .log file in this folder and its subfolders becomes one row. Other files (for example PDF, Word, CSV) are skipped and listed after adding. Inspect the preview, then confirm." Confirm creates one document Data Block with columns `file_path`, `base_name`, `extension`, `document` (same reader as ZIP).
  - Document rule, shared by folders and ZIPs: only `.txt`, `.text`, `.md`, `.rst`, `.log` files become rows (whole file per row); files with other or no extension are skipped (reported as "(no extension)"); non-UTF-8 text is skipped and reported; hidden and OS files (any path part starting with "." such as `.DS_Store`, `.git`, `._*`, or `__MACOSX`) are ignored without being reported. Folder walks are recursive, in path order, never follow symlinks, junctions or reparse points; `file_path` is relative to the chosen folder.
  - Limits: a folder's text files together share the single-file source size limit (error "Folder is too large for node ingestion" / "Folder is too large to preview", HTTP 413); at most 100,000 files ("Folder contains too many files to load").
  - API: node resource gets `skipped_files: [{extension, reason: "unsupported_type" | "not_utf8", count}] | null`, largest groups first.
  - After adding, the success toast "<name> added to project." carries the skip report as its description, shown for 10 seconds, e.g. "50 files skipped while loading: pdf - 30, xlsx - 10, docx - 5, csv - 4, exe - 1"; non-UTF-8 entries show as "txt (not UTF-8) - N". (e6102316 moved it from a separate toast into the success toast's description.)
  - Not in scope: joining a metadata CSV onto documents (load it separately and Join on `base_name`).
  - Tutorial `frontend/public/tutorials/data-loader.md` updated. (The folder add is extended with Texts/Tables modes in #136.)
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Adding a folder containing a.txt, b.md, c.pdf, d.csv, .DS_Store and a subfolder with e.txt creates 3 rows (a, b, sub/e) and a toast description "2 files skipped while loading: csv - 1, pdf - 1" (order by count; ties order not specified in the formatter).
  - A ZIP with a metadata.csv no longer produces a row for it.
  - A symlink inside the folder is ignored.
  - A folder whose text files exceed the source size limit returns 413.
- Implementation: backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py` (`DOCUMENT_EXTENSIONS`, `MAX_DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS`, `SkippedDocumentFiles`, `_DocumentCollector`, `_read_zip_documents`, `_read_directory_documents`, `read_documents`, `DirectoryTooLargeError`, uses `os.scandir` + `is_link_or_reparse`), `services/user_files.py` (`read_path(..., allow_directory=True)` admits real no-follow folders), `services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py`, `models/workspace.py` (`SkippedFileCount`); frontend `data-loader/components/FileTree.tsx`, `AddFilePanel.tsx` (`isFolder`), `DataLoaderFeature.tsx` (`notify` with description, duration 10000ms when described), `hooks/useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts`, `features/workspace/common/skippedFiles.ts` (`describeSkippedFiles`), `useWorkspaceGraphMutations.ts`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_folder_data_block.py` (upload, preview, create end to end), `backend/tests/unit/test_data_loading.py` (rules, links, size limit); frontend `FileTree.test.tsx`, `features/workspace/common/__tests__/skippedFiles.test.ts`, `hooks/__tests__/useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.test.tsx` (single toast with description).
- v0.8 notes: The document table is built with Polars from Python-collected records; DuckDB could do the same via a Python walk (DuckDB `read_text` would not apply the extension/UTF-8/skip rules). Preserve the skip report and the no-link-follow rule (security).

#### V07-020 Sample catalogue prefers size_bytes (#133)

- Commits: 10db38fd
- Area: Data Loader (sample data catalogue)
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: The sample catalogue's Parquet conversion left stale CSV sizes in `size` beside the real counts in `size_bytes`, so every release rejected the catalogue (samples did not list).
- What changed: Data fixed upstream (ldaca-analytics-sample-data 4dc392f sets every `size` to the real byte count, keeps `size_bytes`, and `convert_to_parquet.py` writes both); submodule pointer bumped. `SampleFile` now uses `size_bytes` as `size` when it is present and an int, so a stale `size` cannot break the catalogue again.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A catalogue entry with `size` stale and `size_bytes` correct validates, and the file's size equals `size_bytes`.
  - The Data Loader lists the three sample collections.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/models/data_sources.py` `SampleFile.prefer_size_bytes` (pydantic `model_validator(mode="before")`); `ldaca-analytics-sample-data` submodule.
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_sample_data_integrity.py`.
- v0.8 notes: Alex's DuckDB views may rely on `size_bytes`; `size` is Wordflow's catalogue contract field. Keep both in step.

#### V07-021 LDaCA import: list all collections with access checks, metadata-only import (#135)

- Commits: 54bf549d, ad6148fd
- Area: Data Loader (LDaCA data portal import)
- Type: feature + behaviour change + bug fix
- Problem / reason: The LDaCA dialog asked users to pick a search mode and type a query although the portal holds few top-level collections. Restricted collections could not be imported at all (the collection-level RO-Crate lists no RepositoryObjects, error "RO-Crate contains no RepositoryObject metadata"). Found in app testing: ICE-AUS sections (W1A ... S2B) were flagged top-level but belong to ICE-AUS and have no RO-Crate (404); Australian Deafblind Signing Corpus publishes no item metadata ("lists no objects"); one ICE-AUS text (S1B-065.TXT) has a stray non-UTF-8 byte that failed the whole 1,069-document import.
- What changed:
  - The button/dialog is "Import LDaCA collections". It lists every top-level collection when it opens (new `POST /api/data-portal/collections`, body `{api_token?}`, returns `DataPortalSearchResource`). "Search by" and "Staff Picks" are removed. A "Filter collections" text box (placeholder "Name or description, e.g. COOEE") filters the list locally by name and description. Empty states: "No collections are available." / "No collections match the filter."
  - Only collections without a parent (`_memberOf`) are listed (17 instead of 29); importing ICE-AUS brings every section.
  - Records carry typed access for the current token: `has_access`, `access_group` (from Oni's `_access`), and requests carry `metadata_only`. Each row shows badges: "Restricted: <licence group label>" with a lock icon when the token cannot read it; the published item count ("N items" / "1 item"), or "Collection description only" when 0; and the licence.
  - Restricted rows show the text "Your API token cannot read this collection’s texts. Import its item metadata only, or update your token if you have been granted access." with buttons "Import metadata only" and "Update API token" (opens the credential panel inline; saving re-checks access). A restricted collection with no items shows "Your API token cannot read this collection, and it publishes no item metadata. Import the collection description, or update your token if you have been granted access." with "Import collection metadata". Accessible rows show "Download".
  - Metadata-only import: the worker lists member objects from the search index, fetches each member object's public RO-Crate with bounded concurrency, merges the graphs (entities deduplicated by `@id`), and flattens them with the existing RO-Crate tabulation (LDaCA Tabulator configs). `ldac:speaker` added to the general config's RepositoryObject `expand_props`, so speaker demographics become columns (Sydney Speaks: 261 rows x 52 columns, about 5 s, anonymous).
  - When a crate has no items, tabulation falls back to one row describing the collection, for full and metadata-only imports (Deafblind: 1 row x 32 columns).
  - Invalid UTF-8 bytes in imported texts become U+FFFD instead of failing the import.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated; two old paste-a-URL screenshots removed.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Opening the dialog lists 17 parentless collections without typing; typing "COOEE" filters locally.
  - With no/insufficient token, Sydney Speaks shows "Restricted" and "Import metadata only"; importing gives about 261 rows with speaker columns.
  - ICE-AUS full import gives 1,069 documents; ICE-AUS sections are not listed separately.
  - Deafblind shows "Collection description only" and "Import collection metadata", importing 1 row.
- Implementation: backend `api/data_portal.py` (`list_data_portal_collections`), `services/data_portal.py` (`collections`), `infrastructure/providers/oni.py` (listing, paging, access parsing, parent filter, item counts, object crate fetch), `workers/data_portal.py` (metadata-only merge, one-row fallback, U+FFFD decode), `workers/invocations.py`, `models/data_sources.py` (`DataPortalCollectionsRequest`, `has_access`, `access_group`, `metadata_only`, item count), `domain/user_file_import.py`, `resources/.../general/general-config.json`; frontend `data-loader/components/LdacaImportDialog.tsx`, `hooks/useLdacaImport.ts`, `hooks/ldacaImportState.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `data-portal/components/DataPortalCredentialPanel.tsx`, `providerCredentialRequests.ts`, `tutorials/bundledRegistry.ts`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_oni_client.py` (listing and paging, access parsing, merging object crates, parentless filter, empty-crate fallback, non-UTF-8), `test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `ldacaImportState.test.ts`, `useLdacaImport.test.tsx`, `DataLoaderFeature.test.tsx`, `providerCredentialRequests.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: The Oni portal specifics (`_access`, `_memberOf`, public member crates) are external facts to keep. Tabulation relies on the RO-Crate tabulator, independent of Polars/DuckDB.

#### V07-022 Folder and ZIP batch add: "Texts as one Data Block" or "Tables as separate Data Blocks" (#136)

- Commits: 26f31f8f, 738f77c6
- Area: Data Loader
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users wanted to add a folder (and, per a scope correction by Chao, a ZIP) of table files as separate Data Blocks, not only texts.
- What changed:
  - Adding a folder (the folder row "+" button) or a ZIP opens one dialog (`AddBatchPanel`), titled "Add Folder: <path>" or "Add ZIP: <path>", with tabs (aria-label "Add folder as" / "Add ZIP as"): "Texts as one Data Block" (default) and "Tables as separate Data Blocks (N)" (shows "…" while loading; disabled when there are no table files).
  - Texts description (folder): "Every .txt, .text, .md, .rst and .log file in this folder and its subfolders becomes one row of one Data Block. Other files, including ZIP archives, are skipped and listed after adding." (ZIP): "Every .txt, .text, .md, .rst and .log file in this ZIP becomes one row of one Data Block. Other files, including nested ZIP archives, are skipped and listed after adding." Confirm button "Add to Project".
  - Tables mode: description "Each selected table file becomes its own Data Block, named after the file. Select a file name to preview it."; lists every loadable table file recursively (CSV, TSV, JSON/JSONL, Parquet, Avro, Arrow/IPC, spreadsheets on their first sheet) with per-file checkboxes (aria-label "Add <file>"), "N of M selected", "Select all", "Select none", and a click-to-preview file name (aria-label "Preview <file>"). Confirm button "Add N Data Block(s)" (disabled at 0), "Adding…" while running. Each ticked file becomes its own Data Block named after its stem (relative path shown in the list). One summary toast "N Data Block(s) added to project." and, for failures, an error toast "N file(s) could not be added." with the failed paths as description.
  - A ZIP is never opened as a sub-folder: a folder skips (and in Texts mode reports) contained ZIPs; a ZIP skips nested ZIPs.
  - Folder Tables mode is frontend only (files come from the File Tree, each uses existing single-file creation). ZIP Tables mode adds backend: `GET /api/user-files/zip-tables?path=` returns `{members: [{path, size}]}` (same member safety checks as ZIP loading; hidden/OS members ignored; sorted); preview endpoint takes a `member` query; `FileNodeCreateRequest` gets `zip_member`. The member is extracted to a temporary file (named `member<ext>` so its name cannot escape) within the source/preview size limit ("ZIP member is too large for node ingestion"), then read by the standard loaders; the Data Block is named after the member stem.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A folder with 2 CSVs and 1 XLSX in subfolders shows "Tables as separate Data Blocks (3)"; ticking two and confirming creates two Data Blocks named after the file stems and one success toast.
  - A folder with no table files has the Tables tab disabled.
  - A ZIP containing CSVs lists them under Tables mode; a nested ZIP is not listed.
  - Previewing a ZIP member shows its table.
- Implementation: frontend `data-loader/components/AddBatchPanel.tsx` (replaces `AddFolderPanel.tsx`), `AddFilePanel.tsx`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `hooks/useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts` (`handleAddFilesToWorkspace(paths, zipPath?)`), `hooks/useFilePreview.ts`, `hooks/useZipTableMembers.ts`, `utils/fileTreeHelpers.ts`, `common/hooks/useWorkspaceGraphMutations.ts` (`createNodeFromFile(path, sheet?, zipMember?)`), `lib/queryKeys.ts`; backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py` (`TABLE_EXTENSIONS`, `is_table_path`, `zip_table_members`, `extract_zip_table_member`), `api/files/preview.py` (`list_zip_table_members`, `member` param), `models/files.py` (`ZipTableMember`, `ZipTableMembersResource`), `models/node_resources.py` (`zip_member`), `services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py` (`_load_zip_member_dataframe`).
- Tests: frontend `__tests__/AddBatchPanel.test.tsx` (both modes, Select all/none, previews), `useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.test.tsx` (summary toast), `utils/__tests__/fileTreeHelpers.test.ts`, `lib/__tests__/queryKeys.test.ts`; backend `tests/integration/test_folder_data_block.py` (ZIP tables list, preview, create), `test_current_api_surface.py`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep the two-mode dialog for both folders and ZIPs and the "never descend into ZIPs" rule. Loading uses the standard (Polars) file loaders per file; DuckDB `read_csv`/`read_parquet` etc. would be the analogue.

#### V07-023 File panel: move folders, multi-select, batch delete (#137, #138)

- Commits: 053371a0 (backend), 53f9d310 (frontend)
- Area: Data Loader (user file panel / File Tree)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Folders could not be moved by drag and drop, and users had no way to act on many files at once (deleting was one request per file; there was a dedicated clear-root button).
- What changed:
  - Backend move accepts a real folder and moves it with its contents; moving a folder into itself or a subfolder is rejected ("A folder cannot be moved into itself"); a name clash at the destination stays a conflict (nothing merged or replaced). Folders are renamed under the per-user file gate; files keep hard-link moves.
  - New `POST /api/user-files/batch-delete` body `{paths: [1..10000]}` returns `{deleted: N}`: deletes many files and folders under one gate, covers entries inside a selected folder, skips ones that no longer exist, rejects the root ("The root folder cannot be deleted") and hidden paths.
  - Frontend: folder rows are draggable and move with their contents; empty panel space is a top-level (root) drop target. Drops that would put a folder inside itself are refused; internal drags stop at the row under the pointer so a rejected drop never falls through to the root (OS file drops still reach the upload zone).
  - Multi-select (Finder/Drive/VS Code style): row checkboxes (aria-label "Select <name>") shown on hover and on every row once anything is selected; Shift-click selects a range; Ctrl/Cmd-click toggles; "Select all at root" checkbox (sticky header row; label text "Select all at root" when nothing is selected) and per-folder "Select all in <folder>". The selection bar shows "N selected", a "Move to…" select (aria-label "Move selection to"; options "Root folder" plus valid folders), "Delete" and "Clear". Dragging a selected row moves the whole selection (selection roots); entries already in the target are skipped.
  - Keyboard: Esc clears the selection; Delete/Backspace opens the confirmation.
  - Delete asks once with a count: title "Delete <N files and M folders>?" (e.g. "Delete 312 files and 2 folders?"), description "Folders are deleted with everything inside them. This can’t be undone." (first sentence only when folders are selected), buttons "Cancel" / "Delete". Deletion is permanent (no trash or undo). "Select all at root" then Delete replaces the dedicated clear-root button.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Dragging folder A onto folder B moves A and its contents into B; dragging A onto its own subfolder is refused.
  - Dropping on empty panel space moves to the root.
  - Shift-click selects a contiguous range; Cmd/Ctrl-click toggles one row; Esc clears.
  - Selecting all at root then Delete shows the counted confirmation and removes everything with one batch-delete request.
  - A name clash on move returns a conflict and changes nothing.
- Implementation: backend `api/files/crud.py` (`delete_files` route), `models/files.py` (`BatchDeleteFilesRequest`, `BatchDeleteFilesResource`), `services/user_files.py` (folder move, `delete_many`), `infrastructure/storage/safe_paths.py`; frontend `data-loader/components/FileTree.tsx` (selection state, `selectionRoots`, `canMoveInto`, `describeCount`, bulk delete dialog, props `onMoveMany`, `onDeleteMany`), `hooks/useFileBrowserActions.ts`, `utils/fileTreeHelpers.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`.
- Tests: backend `test_user_file_store.py` (folder move, into-itself guard, conflict, batch delete), `test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `components/__tests__/FileTreeSelection.test.tsx` (folder drops incl. into-itself guard, root drops, Select all at root with counted delete, Shift, Ctrl, Esc, multi-drag, per-folder select all), `FileTree.test.tsx`, `utils/__tests__/fileTreeHelpers.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep server-side guards (into-itself, no merge on clash, root/hidden protection) and the single batch request. Verify drag/drop and Shift/Cmd selection in Safari/WebKit (Chao tests on Safari).

#### V07-024 Download a file selection as one ZIP that keeps its folders (#139)

- Commits: 9d221a68
- Area: Data Loader (user file panel), Export
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users could not download several files or folders at once.
- What changed:
  - The selection bar gains "Download". Selected files and folders become one ZIP whose paths start below the selection's common parent (e.g. top-level one.csv plus speeches/ give `one.csv` and `speeches/...`). ZIP filename: a single selected entry gives "<name>.zip" (a single folder 2020/ downloads as `2020.zip`); several entries give "<common parent name>.zip", or "wordflow_files.zip" when they share only the root. Links and hidden entries are left out; empty selected folders are kept. Deflate compression.
  - Two steps so desktop saves keep streaming natively through the existing GET-only command and long selections never go in a URL: `POST /api/user-files/archives` body `{paths}` validates the selection, checks size against the download limit ("Selection is too large to download as one ZIP", 413), and returns `{id, filename, file_count}` (201), a single-use id valid for 10 minutes (`_ARCHIVE_TTL_SECONDS = 600`); `GET /api/user-files/archives/{id}` builds the ZIP under the user's file gate into a response snapshot and streams it as `application/zip`, cleaning up afterwards.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Selecting a folder `2020/` and clicking Download saves `2020.zip` containing `2020/...` paths relative to the common parent.
  - Reusing the archive id a second time, or after 10 minutes, fails (404).
  - Symlinks and hidden files in selected folders are not in the ZIP; empty folders are.
  - Works in the desktop app without Rust changes.
- Implementation: backend `api/files/crud.py` (`prepare_file_archive`, `download_file_archive`), `models/files.py` (`FileArchiveRequest`, `FileArchiveResource`), `services/user_files.py` (`prepare_archive`, `archive_snapshot`, `_common_parent`, `_archive_filename`, `_archive_members` using `os.walk(followlinks=False)`), `services/response_snapshots.py`; frontend `FileTree.tsx` (`onDownloadMany`), `hooks/useFileBrowserActions.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`.
- Tests: backend `test_user_file_store.py`, `tests/integration/test_folder_data_block.py`, `test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `FileTreeSelection.test.tsx`, `hooks/__tests__/useFileBrowserActions.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep the register-then-GET pattern if the desktop shell only streams GET downloads natively.

#### V07-025 Open/Close projects, Import project, simpler panes, no ids in the graph (#140)

- Commits: 53153282
- Area: Data Loader (Project manager, Active Project pane), Project Graph, Docs
- Type: wording + behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Follow-on simplification after the Workspace to Project rename (#132): Load/Unload and Upload wording was unclear; the Active Project pane's Save only refreshed the project list (every change is already persisted); ids in the graph were noise.

Hint text: "Opening restores the Project’s Data Blocks, Tabs, and analysis history. Choose Open beside the Project you want to continue."

- v0.8 notes: Use Open/Close/Import vocabulary; no manual Save (autosave); no internal ids in the graph UI.

#### V07-069 Data Loader: spreadsheet blocks named after file and sheet (#181)

- Commits: 919cb08c
- Area: Data Loader
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: The backend named a file block `Path(file).stem` and ignored `sheet_name`, so two sheets of one workbook became two blocks with the same name (ANU tester feedback).
- What changed: A block from an Excel-family file (`.xlsx`, `.xls`, `.xlsm`, `.xlsb`, `.ods`, all detected as type `excel`) is named `<file stem>_<sheet>`. When no sheet is chosen, the first sheet's name is used (read with `fastexcel`). `/` and `\` in the sheet name become `-`. A name given by the user still wins. ZIP members are not renamed with a sheet. Files ticked inside a folder add their sheet name too (each is created as its own file request).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - `hansard.xlsx` with no sheet chosen and first sheet "Speeches" gives "hansard_Speeches".
  - With `sheet_name="Speakers_2020"` gives "hansard_Speakers_2020".
  - With `name="My block"` gives "My block".
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/nodes.py` (node creation in `NodeService`, new `_first_sheet_name(path)`), uses `detect_file_type` from `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`. Docs: `tutorials/data-loader.md`.
- Tests: new `backend/tests/integration/test_excel_sheet_node_names.py::test_excel_blocks_are_named_after_file_and_sheet`.
- v0.8 notes: DuckDB's excel extension reads `.xlsx` only; keep the naming rule whatever reader is used. If the first sheet cannot be read the name falls back to the file stem.

- Commits: c31f3432
- Area: Data Loader, Project manager

Wait for it to finish, or cancel it from the task list, before switching projects." to "An analysis is still running in this Project. #### V07-109 Data Loader: say why a file can't be read; quiet no-op type notes (#205 item 20)

- Commits: 31882bc4
- Area: Data Loader (add to Project, file preview), import type normalisation toast, shared Details dialog
- Type: bug fix / wording
- Problem / reason: "User file could not be loaded" and "File preview could not be generated" hid the real reason (not UTF-8, encrypted ZIP, bad CSV and so on). The import toast said "N columns were normalized to standard dtypes" and listed "Int64", "Float32 widened to Float64", "naive datetime assumed UTC".
- What changed:
  - Backend `describe_load_failure(exc, filename)` (`infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`) walks the cause chain and returns, in order of checks:
    - UnicodeError or "utf-8"/"utf8": "The file isn't saved as UTF-8 text. Save it again as UTF-8 (in Excel: CSV UTF-8) and upload it again."
    - "encrypted"/"password": "The ZIP file is password-protected. Unzip it, or save it again without a password, then upload it again."
    - `zipfile.BadZipFile`: "The ZIP file is damaged or incomplete. Create it again and upload it."
    - `pl.exceptions.NoDataError` or "empty": "The file is empty."
    - Excel file or `fastexcel.FastExcelError`: "Wordflow couldn't read this spreadsheet. Open it in Excel, save it again as .xlsx, and upload it again."
    - json/jsonl or "json": "The JSON file isn't valid. Check it with a JSON validator, then upload it again."
    - other `PolarsError`: "Wordflow couldn't read the rows of this file. Check that every row has the same number of columns and that text containing commas is in quotes."
    - fallback: "Wordflow couldn't read this file. Check that it isn't damaged, then try again."
  - Add to Project (`services/nodes.py`): "Couldn't load {filename}. {reason}" with `details.diagnostic`. Preview (`services/file_preview.py`): "Couldn't preview {filename}. {reason}" with `details.diagnostic`. `RuntimeError` (zipfile's password-protected member error) is now in the caught load errors, so it is a 400 with that reason instead of a 500.
  - Normalisation reasons reworded: "naive datetime assumed UTC" to "the times have no time zone, so Wordflow reads them as UTC"; "converted from {tz} to UTC" to "the times were converted from {tz} to UTC"; time precision to "stored to the microsecond (a change you won't see)"; integer promotion to "{kind} whole numbers stored in the standard size, so Data Blocks from different files line up (a change you won't see)"; Float32 widening to "decimals stored in the standard size, so Data Blocks from different files line up (a change you won't see)".
  - Frontend `notifyTypeAdjustments(changes)`: only time-zone changes are announced (reason contains "time zone" or "converted from"). Toast (info, 10 s): "We adjusted 1 column type so Wordflow can use it." / "We adjusted {n} column types so Wordflow can use them.", with a Details link opening a dialog titled "Column types adjusted", explanation "How Wordflow stores these columns. The Data Editor shows each column's type.", lines "{column}: {reason}.", no Send feedback.
  - Shared Details dialog generalised: store holds `{ text, title, explanation, feedback }`; `DetailsDialogButton` exported; errors use title "Error details" with Send feedback.
  - Data Loader notify calls pass the error object as `cause`, so the backend message and Details show (fallbacks like "Failed to add file to project." remain as fallbacks; later reworded by item 25). File preview errors render with `ErrorNotice` (fallback "Couldn't load the preview.").
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Previewing a Latin-1 CSV shows "Couldn't preview x.csv. The file isn't saved as UTF-8 text. …" with Details.
  - Adding a CSV with ragged rows shows "Couldn't load x.csv. Wordflow couldn't read the rows of this file. …".
  - A password-protected ZIP member returns HTTP 400 with the password message.
  - Importing a file with only Int32 columns shows no type toast; a file with naive datetimes shows "We adjusted 1 column type so Wordflow can use it." with Details.
- Implementation: backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`, `services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py`; frontend `components/errors/ErrorDetails.tsx`, `stores/errorDetailsStore.ts`, `features/workspace/common/notifyTypeAdjustments.tsx` (new), `useWorkspaceGraphMutations.ts`, Data Loader hooks (`useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts`, `useFileBrowserActions.ts`, `useFilePreview.ts`, `useFolderCreation.ts`, `useLdacaImport.ts`, `useUploadState.ts`), `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `FilePreviewContent.tsx`.
- Tests: backend `test_canonical_node_api.py`, `test_error_diagnostics.py`, `test_files_preview.py`; frontend `features/workspace/common/__tests__/notifyTypeAdjustments.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: the reason detection matches Polars/fastexcel/zipfile exceptions and message text; DuckDB's CSV/JSON readers raise different errors (for example `InvalidInputException`, "Could not convert string"), so the mapping must be rebuilt for DuckDB while keeping the same user sentences. Type normalisation in v0.8 will differ; still only announce changes users would notice.

#### V07-153 Remove the 64 MiB file size limit for adding and previewing files (#236)

- Commits: 54eb3dfd
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: A single file over 64 MiB could not be previewed or added as a Data Block (`resource_too_large`, HTTP 413), for example a 500 MB Parquet file. The limit was the `max_preview_source_bytes` setting from the v0.7 backend refactor (16 July 2026, first shipped in v0.7.0), shared by preview and ingestion.
- What changed:
  - `FileReadService` and `NodeService` no longer check a single file's size. `FileReadService` takes `max_expanded_bytes` instead of `max_preview_bytes`; `NodeService` drops `max_source_bytes`.
  - Folders of texts and ZIP members still have a bound, because they expand into new bytes (ZIP bombs): the Data Block storage limit, `max_node_storage_bytes` (1 GiB).
  - The `max_preview_source_bytes` setting is removed (`docs/reference/backend-settings.md` updated).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A Parquet file over 64 MiB previews and becomes a Data Block. The real 500 MB, 6.9 million row Hansard `paragraph.parquet` was added in under a second, with a peak of about 4.4 GB extra memory for the eager load.
  - A folder of texts larger than the Data Block storage limit fails with "The folder is too large to add as a Data Block".
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py`, `runtime.py`, `settings.py`.
- Tests: `tests/integration/test_file_size_limits.py` (both cases fail on the old code).
- v0.8 notes: do not reintroduce a per-file source size limit. Stream file ingestion (scan, normalise lazily, sink) so memory stays small whatever the file size; the eager load is the real constraint on small machines.

#### V07-164 Upload limit: none on the desktop, 512 MiB on a server, in plain words (#248)

- Commits: 14680be5
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: `MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_BYTES` (512 MiB, from the 16 July 2026 refactor) applied to the desktop too, because the Data Loader uploads through the same route, so files over 512 MiB could not be added there although #236 removed the other limit. Its messages were "Upload exceeds 536870912 bytes" or "Request body exceeds the configured limit". Decided by Chao: the desktop has no upload limit; a server keeps 512 MiB.
- What changed:
  - `Settings.max_file_upload_bytes` defaults to None; `effective_max_file_upload_bytes()` returns it, else 512 MiB (`DEFAULT_SERVER_MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_BYTES`) when `multi_user`, else None.
  - `UserFileStore.upload(..., declared_bytes=)`: without a limit it reserves the declared `Content-Length` and refuses a body larger than declared; without a declared size it asks to try again.
  - `RequestBodyLimitMiddleware` skips routes whose limit is None and words the upload rejection with `upload_too_large_message`; `UploadTooLargeError` uses the same message.
  - Help: `tutorials/data-loader.md`; `docs/reference/backend-settings.md`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Desktop (single-user): a file over 512 MiB uploads. Server (multi-user): a file over 512 MiB is refused with "This file is larger than the 512 MB upload limit on this server."
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/settings.py`, `services/user_files.py`, `_middleware.py`, `api/files/crud.py`, `shared/errors.py`, `runtime.py`, `main.py`.
- Tests: `test_file_size_limits.py` (mode defaults, plain refusal, desktop upload), `test_user_file_store.py` (declared size, plain message).
- v0.8 notes: no upload limit on the desktop; a configurable limit on multi-user servers, reported in plain words.

#### V07-165 LDaCA loader: Update access token opens Settings at the Portal tab (#249)

- Commits: 62eee0ea
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: In Import LDaCA collections, a restricted collection's Update access token showed an inline token panel above the collection list, easy to miss after scrolling, so the button seemed to do nothing. The token is managed in Settings > Portal.

  - `DataPortalCredentialPanel` calls `notifyPortalTokenChanged` after save or clear; `useLdacaImport` subscribes: reload now if the dialog is open, otherwise invalidate for the next open.

#### V07-171 Uploads: Tasks panel progress, no 30 s timeout, and a live file list (#260)

- Commits: e2fdff3a, 5a36d13f, 07ed0d6a
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: A 460 MB browser upload to the dev server stalled, saved nothing and showed no error, while the Files page sat at "Loading files…". Causes: (1) uploads went through the generated client, whose fetch aborts every request after 30 s, so any browser upload slower than 30 s failed (about 150 MB at 5 MB/s); (2) the upload held the user's file lock while reading the network, so the file list waited for it (94 s at 5 MB/s, 10 min when a proxy stalled); (3) a dropped connection was logged as a 500 with a traceback; (4) upload progress lived inside the Data Loader component and counted files only.
- What changed:
  - Backend: the body streams into the hidden temporary without the per-user lock; the lock is taken only to check the destination and to recheck and publish (`os.link` still refuses to overwrite). `ClientDisconnect` becomes `400 upload_interrupted`, logged at INFO, with the temporary removed.
  - Frontend: `lib/backend/uploadTransport.ts` sends uploads with XMLHttpRequest: byte progress, no overall time limit, stalled after 60 s without progress ("The upload stopped. Your network may be blocking large uploads (common on campus networks); try a smaller file or another network.").
  - `stores/uploadTasksStore.ts` holds uploads outside the Data Loader; each upload action is a Tasks panel task "L - Upload: data.zip" with "180 MB of 460 MB · 5.1 MB/s · about 1 min left" ("file 3 of 24" for several), Stop while running, Clear when finished, and the destination folder as its target.
  - The page asks before a reload or close while an upload runs.
  - Help: `tutorials/data-loader.md`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A slow upload completes however long it takes while bytes move; the user's file list answers during it (0.1 s on the dev server during a 92 s upload).
  - No progress for 60 s fails the upload with the stall message; Stop cancels at once; no partial file is kept.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/user_files.py`, `api/files/crud.py`, `shared/errors.py`; `frontend/src/lib/backend/uploadTransport.ts`, `stores/uploadTasksStore.ts`, `hooks/useUploadLeaveGuard.ts`, `features/views/data-loader/hooks/useUploadState.ts`, `features/workspace/task-stream/taskProjection.ts`, `components/layout/sidebar/SidebarTasksSection.tsx`, `taskRows.ts`.
- Tests: `tests/unit/test_user_file_store.py` (slow upload does not block the list; folder deleted meanwhile), `tests/integration/test_upload_interruption.py`; `uploadTransport.test.ts`, `uploadTasks.test.tsx`, `useUploadState.test.tsx`. Chao tested web uploading on the dev server.
- v0.8 notes: never send uploads through a client with a fixed total timeout; never hold a lock while waiting on network input; a client disconnect is not a server error; work the user starts in the browser belongs in the Tasks panel.

#### V07-172 No fixed upload limit; a reminder before large uploads on a shared server (#248, #260)

- Commits: 9bcd5355
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Decided by Chao on 2026-10-02: an arbitrary per-file cap is worse than telling people what a shared server is for; how users spend their storage quota is their decision. Replaces the 512 MiB server limit of the earlier #248 entry.
- What changed:
  - `Settings.effective_max_file_upload_bytes()` returns only the operator's `MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_BYTES`; the 512 MiB hosted default is removed. Uploads stay bounded by the user's storage quota (refused at the start when larger than the space left) and the free-disk check.
  - In a browser on a remote server (not the desktop app, not localhost), an upload action over 50 MB first shows "This is a large upload (N MB)": the server is for trying out the software, is shared with other people and is not fast enough for a full corpus of this size; install the desktop app (https://sih.tools/wordflow) for large corpora, or Upload anyway. Cancel uploads nothing.
  - Help: `tutorials/data-loader.md`; `docs/reference/backend-settings.md`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - On a hosted server, a file over 512 MiB uploads when the user's quota allows it; a file larger than the space left is refused with `storage_quota_exceeded`.
  - Over 50 MB on a remote server, the reminder appears before anything is created; the desktop app and a local run never show it.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/settings.py`; `frontend/src/features/views/data-loader/utils/largeUploadReminder.ts`, `hooks/useUploadState.ts`, `components/DataLoaderDialogs.tsx`.
- Tests: `test_file_size_limits.py`, `test_multi_user_isolation.py::test_a_hosted_upload_is_bounded_by_the_users_quota_not_a_fixed_cap`; `largeUploadReminder.test.ts`, `largeUploadDialog.test.tsx`, `useUploadState.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: no arbitrary size caps on user content; bound by the user's quota and the server's free space, and inform users about shared-server performance instead of refusing.

#### V07-180 A folder receiving an upload cannot be deleted or moved meanwhile

- Commits: 0df2d255
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Since uploads stream without the per-user lock (#260), a folder could be deleted or moved while an upload wrote into it. On Windows the open temporary file blocks that, so delete failed part way with "Couldn't delete the file." (500). Deleting one file or folder from the tree also failed silently (console only).
- What changed:
  - The User File store tracks the temporaries of uploads in progress per user and refuses, before changing anything, to delete (one or many) or move a folder holding one: 409 "<folder> is receiving an upload. Wait for it to finish, or stop it in the Tasks panel, then try again." The same on every platform.
  - A folder removed outside Wordflow during an upload still fails the upload with "Destination folder not found...".
  - The tree's single delete now shows its error in a notification.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - While a large upload into folder A runs, deleting or moving A is refused with that message and nothing is deleted; after the upload finishes, the delete works.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/user_files.py` (`_active_uploads`, `_require_no_upload_inside`), `frontend/src/features/views/data-loader/hooks/useFiles.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`.
- Tests: `test_user_file_store.py::test_folder_receiving_an_upload_cannot_be_deleted_or_moved`, `::test_upload_into_a_folder_removed_outside_wordflow_fails_cleanly`; `useFiles.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: the Rust server needs the same rule if uploads stream outside a per-user lock.

#### V07-189 Imports: a folder owned by another import is "not published", not an error (#271)

- Commits: 725cd45b
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: When the same collection was imported twice into one folder, the later import's half-published record pointed at a folder owned by the earlier import. At startup `is_import_published` raised, so the import history was marked corrupt ("User File Import history is corrupt" in Tasks, Retry useless) and the record was never cleared, failing again on every restart.
- What changed:
  - `is_import_published` (`services/user_files.py`) answers False when the folder's owner marker names another import, so startup cleans that import's own staging and clears its record.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - With a stale prepared record pointing at a folder another import owns, one restart reads the history normally and the record is gone; the other import's folder is untouched.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/user_files.py`.
- Tests: `test_user_file_store.py`.
- v0.8 notes: recovery checks should treat a folder owned by another import as not published, never as corruption.

#### V07-231 Imports: one unfinishable import no longer marks the whole history corrupt (#310 (follow-up of #271))

- Commits: 77179e05
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: #271 fixed one cause of "User File Import history is corrupt"; any other reconcile failure at startup still failed every history request for that user. Chao moved the follow-up into 0.7.11.
- What changed:
  - `UserFileImportService.start`: a prepared publication or record that cannot be reconciled becomes one unavailable entry ("could not be finished after Wordflow restarted. Remove it from Tasks, then import the files again."), skipped by the records loop; `delete` of such an entry also clears its prepared record.
  - `user_file_import_store._load_user`: files removed between listing and reading are skipped (fixed an intermittent `FileNotFoundError`, about 1 in 25 runs of an existing test).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A broken prepared import leaves the other imports listed; the broken one shows as unavailable and can be removed for good.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/user_file_imports.py`, `infrastructure/storage/user_file_import_store.py`.
- Tests: `test_user_file_import_service.py::test_one_unfinishable_prepared_import_does_not_hide_the_history`.
- v0.8 notes: check v0.8's import history for the same all-or-nothing rule.

#### V07-232 Add selected files and folders as Data Block(s) (#309)

- Commits: dc803d4e
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Chao, 2026-10-05: the file tree could select several items but not add them; first planned for v0.8, then moved into 0.7.11 since a release was due anyway.

The dialog lists the text files instead of a preview.

#### V07-242 Each sheet of a workbook is a table to add, also inside folders, selections and ZIPs (#323)

- Commits: 3e7a32d2
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Chao, 2026-10-07, for 0.7.11: a workbook added one sheet at a time, and workbooks inside a folder, selection or ZIP always added only their first sheet. The folder/ZIP Add window's preview had a Sheet menu that Add ignored (bug), and .xlsm/.ods had no Sheet menu. Decisions: list every sheet as a row in the same tick-to-add window as folders and ZIPs; workbooks in nested subfolders and inside a ZIP the same way; never open a ZIP inside a ZIP or folder, and say so; the preview heading names the table with the hint "Click a table in the list to preview it"; the old single-file sheet picker goes; ZIPs are opened one level only, for cost.
- What changed:
  - Backend `data_loading.py`: `zip_table_members(path, *, max_workbook_bytes)` returns `ZipTableListing(members=[(path, size, sheets|None)], nested_archives=[...])`; a workbook member's sheet names come from reading the member into memory (bounded by the expanded-bytes limit) and `workbook_sheet_names(bytes, suffix)` (validates the workbook's own ZIP container, then `fastexcel.read_excel(...).sheet_names`; no sheet is loaded). `describe_load_failure` says "The sheet is empty." for polars' `empty Excel sheet`.
  - API: `GET /user-files/zip-tables` members gain `sheets` (null for non-workbooks or unreadable workbooks) and the resource gains `nested_archives`. New `POST /user-files/workbook-sheets` `{paths}` (1 to 1,000) returning `WorkbookSheetsResource {workbooks: [{path, sheets, error}]}` (an unreadable workbook reports its error with no sheets). `GET /user-files/preview` passes `sheet_name` through for a ZIP member. Node creation passes `sheet_name` to a ZIP member (`_load_zip_member_dataframe`) and names it `<stem>_<sheet>` when a sheet is given.
  - Frontend: `utils/batchTables.ts` (`BatchTableFile {id, label, path, sheet?}`, `expandSheets(sources, sheetsOf)`: a workbook with two or more sheets gives one row per sheet labelled `file › Sheet`, id `path\u0000sheet`; one sheet or unreadable gives one row with no sheet). `useWorkbookSheets(paths)` / `workbookSheetsQuery`. `fileTreeHelpers`: `isWorkbookPath` (xlsx, xls, xlsm, xlsb, ods), `archivesInDirectory`, `filesInSelection(...).archives`. `AddBatchPanel`: `BatchSource.kind` adds `workbook` (title "Add Workbook: path", description "Each ticked sheet becomes its own Data Block, named after the file and the sheet.", no Texts tab); `skippedArchives` note "N ZIP archive(s) inside are not opened: a.zip, b.zip. Add each ZIP on its own."; preview heading "Preview: <row label>" with hint "Click a table in the list to preview it." (when more than one row); `onConfirmTables(files)` receives the rows. `useFilePreview(path, open, member, fixedSheet)`: a fixed sheet previews that sheet with no Sheet menu; the Sheet menu's workbook test now covers .xlsm and .ods. `AddFilePanel` has no Sheet menu. Row **Add** on a workbook reads its sheet names first: two or more open the workbook window, otherwise the single-file window. `handleAddFilesToWorkspace(files, zipPath?)` passes each row's sheet and reports failures as `label: reason`, heading "N table(s) could not be added."
  - Help: data-loader.md (Tables mode, row Add, selection Add, nested ZIP note).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Row Add on survey.xlsx (Responses, Codes, Empty): "Add Workbook: survey.xlsx" lists three rows; the preview shows Responses, clicking Codes previews code/label; ticking Responses and Empty adds survey_Responses and reports "1 table could not be added. survey.xlsx › Empty: Couldn't load survey.xlsx. The sheet is empty." (Playwright WebKit).
  - ZIP with data/survey.xlsx and old/inner.zip: Tables lists data/survey.xlsx › Responses, › Codes, › Empty, the note names old/inner.zip, Codes previews and adds as survey_Codes.
  - Folder with 2021/wave1.xlsx, list.csv and archived.zip: Tables lists 2021/wave1.xlsx › Responses, › Codes, › Empty and list.csv; the note names archived.zip.
- Implementation: Backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`, `services/file_preview.py`, `api/files/preview.py`, `models/files.py`, `services/nodes.py`. Frontend `features/views/data-loader/` (`DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `AddBatchPanel.tsx`, `AddFilePanel.tsx`, `FilePreviewContent.tsx`, `useFilePreview.ts`, `useZipTableMembers.ts`, `useWorkbookSheets.ts`, `useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts`, `utils/batchTables.ts`, `utils/fileTreeHelpers.ts`), `lib/queryKeys.ts`.
- Tests: `test_folder_data_block.py::test_workbook_sheets_are_listed_and_added_one_by_one` (batch sheet names with an unreadable workbook, ZIP members with sheets and nested archives, ZIP member sheet preview and add, empty sheet message); API surface; `AddBatchPanel.test.tsx` (workbook rows, preview heading and hint, nested ZIP note); `batchTables.test.ts`; `fileTreeHelpers.test.ts`; `useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.test.tsx` (sheets passed, failure reasons); Playwright WebKit run of all three cases.
- v0.8 notes: Keep the tick-to-add window as the one way to add several tables, with one row per sheet of a multi-sheet workbook, in workbooks on their own, in folders (with subfolders), selections and ZIPs. Open ZIPs one level only and name skipped nested ZIPs. List sheet names without loading sheets (calamine/fastexcel sheet names, or DuckDB's spatial/excel reader metadata). Name a sheet's Data Block `<file>_<sheet>`. Report an empty sheet in plain words.

#### V07-270 LDaCA import with access reads each item's text (Sydney Speaks) (#351)

- Commits: 9e7dbf41
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Monika (2026-10-08, with her key): importing Sydney Speaks gave one row, the collection's description. The full import read only the collection's own crate and kept only text/plain files listed there; Sydney Speaks keeps a transcript CSV in each of its 261 items, named by ldac:indexableText. The metadata-only import walked the items (261 rows). Chao: no per-collection code; follow the curators' standard terms.
- What changed:
  - When the collection's crate lists no text, the full import walks every RepositoryObject, reads the file each names as ldac:mainText, else ldac:indexableText (falling back to plain-text files), plain text and CSV only.
  - Plain text: one row; CSV: one row per CSV row, all columns as text (one per utterance for a transcript). Item metadata joins every row as item_* columns (item_id, item_file, ...); items without text keep one row. Shared download budget; per-item progress.
  - Help (data-loader.md) explains the item text.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Sydney Speaks with access: one row per utterance from 261 speaker CSVs, with item_* speaker metadata.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/workers/data_portal.py` (`_item_text_files`, `_fetch_item_texts`, `_write_item_texts`, `_metadata_table`).
- Tests: `test_data_portal_items.py` (term priority, fallback, CSV stacking, mocked portal fetch); live check of Sydney Speaks' public metadata (261 items, CSV per item; downloads need a key).
- v0.8 notes: Import by the standard LDaCA terms (mainText/indexableText), walk items, stack tabular transcripts; per-collection text-column metadata (CSVW) belongs to curators.

### Data Editor

#### V07-026 Delete several columns at once (#141)

- Commits: 82831184
- Area: Data Editor (then still called Data view)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users could only delete one column at a time from the column menu; each deletion was a separate Undo step.
- What changed: A "Delete columns" button (icon Columns3, title "Delete several columns at once") sits before Undo in the Data view header. It opens a dialog titled "Delete columns" with description "Choose the columns to remove from this Data Block. Undo restores them in one step.", a "Filter columns" input, a "{n} of {total} selected" count, "Select all" (adds the currently visible, filtered columns) and "Select none" buttons, a scrolling checkbox list ("No columns match the filter." when empty), and a red confirm button "Delete {n} column(s)". If every column is ticked, an alert "Keep at least one column." shows and the confirm button is disabled. The button is disabled when the block has fewer than 2 columns. Backend adds a `delete_columns` edit so the batch is one Data Block Edit and one Undo.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Ticking 3 columns and confirming removes all 3; one Undo restores all 3.
  - Ticking all columns disables the confirm button and shows "Keep at least one column."; the backend also refuses with "Keep at least one column on the Data Block".
  - Unknown column names are rejected with "Columns to delete are not present on the Data Block: a, b"; duplicate names in the request are rejected ("Columns to delete must be unique").
  - Select all with a filter active only ticks the filtered columns.
  - Closing the dialog clears the selection and filter.
- Implementation: backend `DeleteColumnsNodeEditRequest` (kind `delete_columns`, `columns` 1..10000 unique) in `models/node_resources.py`; `build_edited_lazyframe` in `services/node_operations.py` does `node.data.drop(columns)`. Frontend `DeleteColumnsDialog.tsx`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx` (`onDeleteColumns`), `useWorkspaceTransformMutations.deleteColumns`, `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`. Tutorial `ui.md`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_delete_columns_edit.py::test_delete_columns_is_one_edit_and_keeps_a_column`; `frontend/.../data-view/components/__tests__/DeleteColumnsDialog.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep "one batch = one undo step" and the "at least one column remains" rule (a block with zero columns cannot keep its rows). Pure column drop; DuckDB equivalent is a projection without those columns.

#### V07-027 Data Editor: in-place column tools move out of Preprocessing, with live edit preview (#143)

- Commits: d13d63ab (backend), 7ca8d6d3 (frontend)
- Area: Data Editor, Data Builder (Preprocessing), Docs
- Type: feature / behaviour change
- Problem / reason: After #129, Preprocessing tools always created new blocks, while its Find and Create sub-tabs always edited the selected block in place. The in-place single-block column operations belong with other per-block edits (rename, cast, delete columns) in the table itself, as in spreadsheets and OpenRefine. Feedback also said "Data view" suggests inspection only.
- What changed:
  - The Data view header now reads "Data Editor" (help label "Data Editor"). Toolbar: "Add column" dropdown ("Combine columns…", "Duplicate column…", "Extract text…", "Split column…"), "Find & replace" button, "Clean text" dropdown (one item per cleaning operation), "Delete columns", "Undo", "Redo". The header toolbar is `flex-wrap`.
  - Each column header's settings menu gains "Find & replace…", "Clean text…", "Extract text…", "Split…", "Duplicate…" (column pre-filled) above Rename/Delete.
  - A tool opens a panel in place of the Project Graph (top-right). Header: tool label " · <block name>", a "Show Project Graph" button, and a close button ("Close tool"). While the graph is shown, a bar "Back to <tool label>" returns to the tool. The panel is kept mounted (hidden) while the graph is shown.
  - While the form is valid the table below shows a server preview of the edited page; added or changed columns are tinted (header `bg-button/20`, cells `bg-button/10`, `data-preview-column` attribute). Footer status: "Complete the settings to preview the result below." / "Previewing…" / "N row(s) changed" (whole block). "Apply" commits one Data Block Edit (toast "<tool> applied to <block>."); "Cancel" discards.
  - Switching to another Data Block: if the tool is untouched it closes silently; if touched, an alert "Discard your unfinished <tool> on <block>?" with description "The settings have not been applied. Keep editing to return to that Data Block." and buttons "Keep editing" (re-selects and activates the tool's block) / "Discard". Drafts are never kept per block.
  - Tool labels: "Find & replace", "Extract text", "Combine columns", "Duplicate column", "Clean text", "Split column".
  - Duplicate: no options; copy named Finder-style "<col> copy", "<col> copy 2", ... and inserted right of the original. Panel text: 'The copy is placed right of the original and named like a copied file, for example "<col> copy".'
  - Clean text operations (labels): "Trim spaces at the start and end", "Collapse repeated spaces", "lowercase", "UPPERCASE", "Title Case", "Remove punctuation and symbols", "Remove digits", "Remove web links (URLs)", "Remove HTML tags". Output: "Save result to" radio "The same column" / "A new column, right of it" (+ name box).
  - Split column (first version): "Split on" text, "Number of columns" 2..50; outputs `<col>_1..<col>_n` right of the source, last keeps the remainder. (Reworked in #146 and #163.)
  - Find & replace / Extract text (first version, regex only): labels "Find (regular expression)", "Replace with", "Only the first match in each row"; Extract: "Extract matches of (regular expression)", "Join several matches with" (default " "), "New column name". Find's Extract output now sits right of its source.
  - Combine columns (first version: ordered checkbox list + "Separator"), replaced the same day by the template (next entry).
  - Preprocessing keeps only Filter, Sample, Join and Stack; the Find and Create sub-tabs, their contextual hints ("Transform text with a pattern", "Build an analysis-ready column", "Your Data Block was updated") and help entries are removed; `ui.md` and `preprocessing.md` point to the Data Editor.
  - New endpoint `POST /workspaces/{ws}/nodes/{id}/edits/preview?page&page_size` (page_size 1..500, default 50) runs any `NodeEditRequest` without applying it, returns an Arrow page plus header `X-Wordflow-Changed-Rows` (exposed via CORS).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Duplicating "text" twice gives "text copy" then "text copy 2", each directly right of "text".
  - Clean text into a new column places it right of the source; into the same column replaces values in place; an existing output name is refused ("Output column name already exists on the Data Block").
  - Split refuses if any `<col>_i` already exists ("Split would overwrite existing columns: ...").
  - The preview never changes the stored block; Undo after Apply restores the previous plan.
  - "N rows changed" counts rows where an edited column (a column named in the request) differs (null-aware) or a new column is non-null and non-empty, over the whole block, not just the page.
  - Selecting another block with a touched tool shows the Keep editing / Discard prompt; with an untouched tool, the tool just closes.
  - Preprocessing shows only Filter, Sample, Join, Stack tabs.
- Implementation: backend request models `DuplicateColumnNodeEditRequest`, `CleanTextNodeEditRequest` (`CleanTextOperation` literal), `SplitColumnNodeEditRequest` in `models/node_resources.py`; `build_edited_lazyframe`, `duplicate_column_name`, `_place_right_of` (pure `select` reorder), `_clean_text_expression` in `services/node_operations.py`; `NodeService.preview_edit`, `_preview_edit`, `_count_changed_rows` (casts compared columns to String, `ne_missing`, `any_horizontal(...).sum()`) in `services/nodes.py`; route `preview_node_edit` in `api/workspaces/nodes.py`; `CHANGED_ROWS_HEADER` in `shared/table_transport.py`. Frontend: zustand store `dataEditorToolStore.ts` (tool, nodeId, request, highlightColumns, changedRows, dirty, graphVisible), pure builders `dataEditorRequests.ts` (`buildFindReplace`, `buildExtract`, `buildDuplicate`, `buildCleanText`, `buildSplit`, `duplicateColumnName`), `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`, `WorkspaceView.tsx` (swaps the graph for the panel), `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx`, `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx` (`COLUMN_TOOLS`), `WorkspaceTable.tsx` (highlight), `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts` (preview query with `placeholderData` keep-previous, switch guard), `api/tableApi.ts::previewNodeEditTable`. Removed `preprocessing/aggregate/*` and `preprocessing/replace/*`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_data_editor_edits.py` (`test_preview_reports_changed_rows_without_applying`, `test_duplicate_split_and_clean_apply_as_single_edits`), `backend/tests/unit/test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `data-view/__tests__/dataEditorRequests.test.ts`, `data-view/components/__tests__/DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx`, updated `guidance/__tests__/registry.test.tsx`, `DataPreprocessingFeature.test.tsx`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: The UX contract to preserve: one tool open at a time, preview in the real table with highlighted columns, whole-block changed-row count, Apply = one undoable edit, discard prompt on block switch, new columns inserted right of their source. The changed-row count is a Polars lazy comparison; in DuckDB it is a single `SELECT count(*) WHERE a IS DISTINCT FROM b OR ...` over the edited projection. Punctuation removal uses Rust-regex Unicode classes `[\p{P}\p{S}]`; DuckDB's RE2 supports `\pP`/`\pS` but check syntax. Title Case in v0.7 is a workaround (see Title Case entry) that is not needed in DuckDB.

#### V07-028 Combine columns uses a template with column suggestions (#143 follow-up)

- Commits: 8dd3d9f3
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature / behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Feedback on the first Combine: an ordered checkbox list with one separator could not put different text between columns, and long column lists needed scrolling.
- What changed: Combine columns is a "Template" textarea such as `{title}: {body}` (placeholder "e.g. {title}: {body}", monospace). Typing "{" opens a "Matching columns" listbox (prefix matches first, then substring matches, max 8); ArrowUp/Down move, Enter or Tab inserts, Escape dismisses; a closing "}" already present is skipped. A searchable "Insert column" picker (search placeholder "Find a column… (* and ? wildcards)") inserts at the caret. `{{` and `}}` are literal braces. Help line: "Type { to pick a column, or use Insert column. Other text is kept as written." / "Uses a, b. Write {{ or }} for a literal brace." Errors: "Write }} for a literal closing brace.", "A { has no matching }.", "Put a column name inside the braces.", "Not a column on this Data Block: x". Plus "New column name" and "When a value is missing": "Treat it as blank text" (default) / "Leave the combined value empty". The panel shows "First row on this page: "<value>"" (or italic "empty"). Every Data Editor tool's column picker becomes a searchable select. When opened from a column menu the template starts as `{<col>}`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - `{title}: {body}` on a row title="A", body="B" gives "A: B".
  - Columns of any type (numbers, dates) join as text.
  - With "Treat it as blank text", a null column contributes ""; with "Leave the combined value empty", any null part makes the result null.
  - A template with no column reference, an unknown column, or an unbalanced brace does not preview and Apply is disabled.
  - The new column name must be new; backend refuses existing names ("New column name already exists on the Data Block").
  - The combined column is appended at the end of the block (not right of a source).
- Implementation: backend `CombineTextPart`, `CombineColumnPart`, `CombineColumnsNodeEditRequest` (kind `combine_columns`, `parts` 1..200, `output_column`, `empty_values` "blank" | "empty_result") in `models/node_resources.py`; `pl.concat_str` of `pl.col(c).cast(String)` and `pl.lit(text)` with optional `fill_null("")` in `node_operations.py`. Frontend `CombineTemplateField.tsx` (combobox ARIA pattern), `parseCombineTemplate`, `templateColumnToken`, `buildCombine` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`; `previewSample` in `dataEditorToolStore.ts` and `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_combine_columns_template_handles_types_and_missing_values`; `dataEditorRequests.test.ts`, `DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: `concat_str` without `ignore_nulls` returns null if any part is null; DuckDB `concat` skips nulls while `||` propagates them, so pick the operator to match each "missing value" option. Keep the template grammar (braces, doubled braces as literals) so saved user habits carry over.

#### V07-029 Title Case edits survive Project save and load (#143 follow-up)

- Commits: 99f475d7
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: `str.to_titlecase` saved fine but polars-source-utils could not read the plan back ("unknown variant Titlecase"), so reopening the Project failed.
- What changed: Title Case is rebuilt from supported ops: mark each word (`\w+(?:['’]\w+)*`) with a separator, split, uppercase the first character and lowercase the rest of each piece, join. Apostrophes inside words are kept ("It's" not "It'S").
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - "it's a TEST" becomes "It's A Test".
  - A Project containing a Title Case edit reopens without error.
  - Every Data Editor edit kind serializes and reads back through polars-source-utils.
- Implementation: `_title_case` in `services/node_operations.py` (uses `_SPLIT_MARK = "\x1f"` and `list.eval`).
- Tests: new `backend/tests/unit/test_data_editor_saved_plans.py` (`test_edit_plan_can_be_read_back` parametrised over every edit, `test_title_case_keeps_apostrophes_inside_words`).
- v0.8 notes: Polars/polars-source-utils specific workaround; DuckDB can implement Title Case directly (e.g. regex or list functions) but keep the apostrophe rule. The "every plan must round-trip" regression test is the important idea to carry over for whatever v0.8 persists.

#### V07-031 Find & replace and Extract text match plain text by default (#145)

- Commits: 980f492d (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Many users do not know regular expressions and mistakes are silent: typing "." to remove the dot before a file extension matched every character and emptied the column.
- What changed: Labels become "Find" and "Extract matches of" with a "Use regular expression" checkbox (unchecked by default). Placeholders: plain "e.g. .txt" / "e.g. #auspol"; regex "e.g. \s+" / "e.g. #\w+". In plain mode the pattern is literal and "$" in the replacement is literal (no `$1` group references). Requests carry `literal: true`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Find "." replace "" on "a.txt" gives "atxt" in plain mode; with regex ticked it gives "".
  - Replacement "$1" in plain mode inserts the text "$1".
  - Extract "#auspol" in plain mode extracts only that literal text.
  - Older saved plans without the flag keep regex behaviour.
- Implementation: `ReplaceDerivation.literal: bool = False` in `domain/workspace/provenance.py`; `_replace_expression` uses `str.replace_all(..., literal=)`, `str.replace(..., literal=)`, and `pl.escape_regex` for extract. Frontend `RegexOption` in `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`; `buildFindReplace`/`buildExtract` set `literal: !regex`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_count_and_plain_text_replace`; `dataEditorRequests.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: Default must stay plain text. Backward compatibility: absent flag means regex. Polars regex is Rust `regex`; DuckDB uses RE2 (no backreferences in patterns, replacement group syntax `\1` rather than `$1`), so regex-mode semantics may differ slightly.

#### V07-032 Split column on several delimiters, from the left or the right (#146)

- Commits: 980f492d (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Extend Split column like spreadsheet Text to Columns and Python split/rsplit.
- What changed: Request has `delimiters` (1..20 unique plain-text strings) and `direction` "left" | "right". UI at this commit: "Split on any of" checkboxes Comma (default ticked), Semicolon, Space, Tab, "Pipe ( | )", New line, plus "Other text" (placeholder "e.g. :: or  - "); "Split from" radios "The left (the last column keeps the rest)" / "The right (the first column keeps the rest)". Help: "Text left over after the split keeps its original delimiters." (Checkboxes replaced by chips in #163.)
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - "a,b;c d" with delimiters [",", ";"] into 2 columns from the left gives "a" and "b;c d".
  - From the right into 2 columns gives "a,b" and "c d" (remainder keeps delimiters); a short row fills from the left as Python rsplit does.
  - When two delimiters match at the same position, the longest wins (", " over ",").
  - Leading or trailing delimiters produce empty parts; there is no "treat consecutive delimiters as one" option.
  - Missing parts are null.
- Implementation: `_delimiter_pattern` (escaped alternation, longest first), `_split_pieces` (insert `\x1f` after each delimiter, split), `_split_parts` (list.get / slice / join, strips trailing delimiter per part) in `node_operations.py`; `SplitColumnNodeEditRequest` in `node_resources.py`; `buildSplit` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_split_on_several_delimiters_from_either_side`; saved-plan round trip for both directions.
- v0.8 notes: The marker-character trick is a Polars workaround to keep which delimiter was used; in DuckDB, `regexp_split_to_array` loses delimiters, so a different approach is needed to keep the remainder's original delimiters. Preserve the rsplit fill rule.

#### V07-033 Count tool: words, characters, or matches (#147)

- Commits: 980f492d (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users wanted a count column without writing expressions.
- What changed: New tool "Count" in Add column ("Count words, characters, or matches…") and in each column menu ("Count…"). "Count" radios: "Words", "Characters", "Characters, not counting spaces", "Matches of a text or pattern" (then "Text to count" with placeholder "e.g. !" or regex "e.g. \bthe\b", and "Use regular expression"). Note: "Words are runs of text between spaces or line breaks, as word processors count them." "New column name" defaults (placeholder, used when left blank) to "<col> word count", "<col> character count", "<col> character count (no spaces)", "<col> match count". Output placed right of the source.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - "Hello  big world" gives Words 3, Characters 16, Characters not counting spaces 13.
  - Matches of "!" (plain) on "Hi!!" gives 2.
  - A null text gives a null count, not 0.
  - Leaving the name blank creates "text word count" for column "text".
- Implementation: `CountNodeEditRequest` (kind `count`, measure, pattern, regex, output_column) in `node_resources.py`; `_count_expression` (`count_matches(r"\S+")`, `len_chars`, whitespace removal, `count_matches(literal=not regex)`) in `node_operations.py`; `COUNT_MEASURES`, `defaultCountName`, `buildCount` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_count_and_plain_text_replace`; saved-plan test covers count.
- v0.8 notes: Keep null-in, null-out. Character counts are Unicode characters (`len_chars`), not bytes; DuckDB `length()` counts characters, `strlen()` bytes.

#### V07-042 Data Editor: scroll the preview column into view; keep focus in the tool panel (#154)

- Commits: d8ff5f1e, af7ffed8 (scroll part), 69c5be2c, da5fa715
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: behaviour change / bug fix (Safari)
- Problem / reason: On a wide block the highlighted preview column could be off-screen. Follow-ups: opening a tool from a menu handed focus back to the menu trigger after the first keystroke, so typing a new column name stopped after one letter; after picking a "{" suggestion, a key typed immediately landed before it ("{a} + " became "{a}+ "); the Chromium-only fixes did not hold in Safari.
- What changed (final state):
  - While a tool previews, the table scrolls so the source column (`scrollAnchor`) sits at the left edge of the table (after left-pinned columns), so the changed or new column beside it shows. Combine columns (no source) scrolls to the far right end, where it adds its column. Every new preview (any settings change, keyed by the JSON request) realigns; paging through a preview keeps the position.
  - Safari workaround: for up to 1500 ms after aligning, the alignment is reapplied when the table or viewport resizes (ResizeObserver) or the scroll position drifts by more than 1px, until the user wheels, presses a pointer, or presses a key inside the scroll area.
  - Tool menus (header dropdowns and column menus) cancel Radix's `onCloseAutoFocus` and call `focusDataEditorTool()`, which focuses the first field of `[data-editor-tool-form]` (textarea, text input, or combobox button, excluding "Insert column"); the panel also calls it on mount. If focus is already inside the panel (Safari runs the menu close callback late), it does nothing.
  - Combine template: caret after an autocomplete insertion is set in `useLayoutEffect` in the same commit (no timer); the Insert column picker path keeps the delayed focus because focus is in its popup.
  - History: d8ff5f1e first aligned the last highlighted column to the right edge only when the highlighted set changed; af7ffed8 made it realign on every preview; da5fa715 switched to source-at-left-edge (as Chao proposed) and added the Safari settling window.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Clean text on "text" in a wide block: "text" is at the left edge of the viewport after the preview arrives.
  - Duplicate: source at the left edge, copy visible beside it.
  - Combine columns: table scrolled to the end, new column visible.
  - Scroll away, change an option: the alignment comes back; change page: it does not.
  - Open a tool from a menu and type a name quickly: every character lands in the field (verified in WebKit and Chromium).
  - Picking "{user_id}" from suggestions then typing " + " gives "{user_id} + ".
- Implementation: `WorkspaceTable.tsx` (`previewScroll` prop `{key, column}`, `scrolledKeyRef`, `stopSettlingRef`, ResizeObserver, listeners on `[data-slot="scroll-area"]`), `dataEditorRequests.ts` (`scrollAnchor` per builder: source column, `null` for combine), `dataEditorToolStore.ts` (`scrollAnchor`), `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`, new `focusDataEditorTool.ts`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx` (`keepToolFocus`), `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx` (`openedToolRef`), `CombineTemplateField.tsx` (`pendingCaretRef`), `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx` (`data-editor-tool-form`).
- Tests: `data-view/components/__tests__/WorkspaceTable.test.tsx` (scroll cases), `DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx`, `dataEditorRequests.test.ts`; manual Playwright WebKit and Chromium runs.
- v0.8 notes: WebKit-specific: Safari moves scrollLeft back while re-rendering rows and settles column widths late, and runs Radix menu close callbacks late. Any v0.8 table that auto-scrolls must be verified in Playwright WebKit (Chao uses Safari). Keep "user interaction cancels auto-alignment".

#### V07-043 Clean text: Remove XML tags and markup (#155)

- Commits: af7ffed8 (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users with XML corpora need to strip markup and keep text; "Remove HTML tags" (`<[^>]+>`) breaks on quoted attributes containing ">" and leaves entities.
- What changed: New Clean text option "Remove XML tags and markup" (`remove_xml_tags`). Removes element tags (namespaced names, quoted attributes that may contain ">", self-closing), XML declarations and processing instructions (`<?...?>`), comments, DOCTYPE with or without an internal subset, and CDATA wrappers (keeping inner text); then decodes `&lt; &gt; &quot; &apos; &amp;` (with "&amp;" last so "&amp;lt;" becomes "&lt;"). Sequential Analysis info page now points to Clean text instead of a regex.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - `<?xml version="1.0"?><!-- c --><a:b x="1>2">Tom &amp; Jerry</a:b><![CDATA[x<y]]>` becomes `Tom & Jerry x<y` (no separator inserted; exact spacing follows the input).
  - The plan survives Project save and load.
- Implementation: `_XML_MARKUP_PATTERN`, `_XML_ENTITIES`, branch in `_clean_text_expression` in `node_operations.py`; `CLEAN_TEXT_OPERATIONS` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_saved_plans.py::test_remove_xml_tags_keeps_text_and_decodes_entities` and round trip.
- v0.8 notes: The pattern uses `[\s\S]*?` lazy quantifiers, supported by both Rust regex and RE2. Numeric character references (`&#123;`) are not decoded.

#### V07-049 Split column: typed delimiter chips; Number of columns beside Column (#163)

- Commits: bb61d219
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: "Number of columns" sat below Split from and scrolled out of the panel; the preset checkboxes plus "Other text" were clumsy.
- What changed: "Delimiters" chip input (default chip ","): type punctuation, a space, or several characters and press Enter to add; duplicates ignored; "x" ("Remove delimiter <label>") or Backspace in the empty field removes the last chip. Placeholder "Type a delimiter, then Enter" / "Add another"; help "Press Enter after each delimiter: punctuation, a space, or several characters." A lone space shows as "space", a lone tab as "tab", and spaces/tabs inside longer delimiters as "␣"/"⇥" (". " shows as ".␣"). One predefined checkbox "Also split at each new line". "Number of columns" (2..50) sits beside "Column"; "Split from" radios on one line.

  - Backspace in the empty input removes the last chip.
  - Ticking "Also split at each new line" adds "\n" to the request delimiters.

#### V07-050 Data Editor: new-column options preview under a default name (#164)

- Commits: 27663b1c (7c6879b2 fixed the Tab race it exposed; see #156 entry)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Choosing "A new column, right of it" in Find & replace showed no preview ("Complete the settings…") because the name started empty, and the name box sat below the option, out of view on a short panel.
- What changed: New-column names default (placeholder, used when blank) to "<col> replaced" (Find & replace), "<col> cleaned" (Clean text), "<col> matches" (Extract text), and preview straight away; Tab accepts the default for editing. The name box shares the option's line.

  - Find "_" in "username" with A new column previews a "username replaced" column right of "username"; typing a name renames it.
  - Extract text previews immediately under "<col> matches" once a pattern is entered.
